### [1] [Emergence of the SARS-CoV-2 furin cleavage site in humans is constrained to late 2019: implications for COVID-19 origins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20416)
*Steven E Massey*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本研究通过等待时间计算法，利用经验确定的参数，估算SARS-CoV-2刺突蛋白中弗林蛋白酶切割位点（FCS）插入与其补偿性突变D614G出现之间的时间间隔为37-65天，由此推断FCS出现的最晚时间约为2019年10月28日至11月25日，与系统发育分析得出病毒在2019年末出现的时间线一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SARS-CoV-2刺突蛋白中的FCS插入是其他sarbecovirus所没有的特征，与其在人类中的传播性有关，但其起源（自然产生还是人工插入）一直存在争议，需要定量方法来区分不同的病毒出现场景。

Method: 利用FCS对刺突蛋白的失稳效应及D614G补偿性突变这一已知关系，结合一系列经验确定的生物学参数，进行等待时间（waiting time）计算，估算从FCS出现到D614G突变产生之间的时间间隔。

Result: 等待时间估算范围为37至65天。基于D614G于2020年1月1日最早被检测到，推算FCS出现的最晚日期为2019年10月28日至11月25日，与系统发育分析得出的病毒出现时间一致。

Conclusion: FCS的出现与SARS-CoV-2的流行浪潮在时间上吻合。该方法排除了某些出现场景：含FCS的前体病毒在人类群体中于2019年末之前隐蔽传播的可能性不大，而含FCS的前体病毒在蝙蝠中长期传播也与缺乏补偿性突变的事实不符。

Abstract: SARS-CoV-2 possesses a furin cleavage site (FCS) insertion in its spike protein, that is not found in other sarbecoviruses, and is responsible for its transmissibility in humans. The origin of the FCS insertion is unclear, and has been a point of debate regarding whether it arose naturally, or represents an artificial insertion. The FCS is destabilizing to spike protein, and as a consequence a compensatory mutation, D614G, rapidly emerged during the early pandemic and swept to fixation. Using a range of empirically determined parameters, a waiting time calculation can be used to determine the time interval between the appearance of the FCS and generation of the compensating D614G mutation. The waiting time is estimated to range from 37 to 65 days. Given the earliest detection of D614G on 1 January 2020, the latest dates for the appearance of the FCS range from October 28 to November 25 2019. This timeline is consistent with dates generated from phylogenetic considerations for the emergence of SARS-CoV-2 in late 2019, indicating that FCS appearance and virus emergence correspond. The waiting time approach enables discrimination between various scenarios of SARS-CoV-2 emergence, in particular cryptic circulation of a FCS-containing progenitor in the human population prior to late 2019 is unlikely, while prolonged circulation of a FCS-containing progenitor in bats appears inconsistent with absence of the compensatory mutation.

</details>


### [2] [A framework for combined epidemiological-genomic inference to improve estimation of household model parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21094)
*Golsa Sayyar,Joe Hilton,Thomas House*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于连续时间马尔可夫链的家庭传播模型，通过将模型状态空间扩展至包含传播图（记录感染方向与顺序），并结合病原体基因组数据来识别兼容的传播历史，从而大幅提高家庭内外部传播参数的识别能力。模拟研究表明，基因组数据能显著集中似然区域、减少内外传播参数之间的依赖，消除弱可识别性带来的脊状结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的家庭结构传染病模型利用终态数据（final-size data）校准，但终态数据因忽略传播顺序而无法区分家庭内部与外部传播，导致参数可识别性受限，难以准确推断内外传播率。

Method: 开发一个连续时间马尔可夫链（CTMC）公式来描述家庭传播动态，将模型状态空间扩展为包含传播图（描述感染方向和顺序）；用理想化的病原体基因组数据来约束与观测兼容的传播历史，并通过模拟研究比较仅用流行病学数据与结合基因组数据的似然面。

Result: 模拟研究表明，纳入基因组信息能显著集中高似然区域；基因组数据降低了内部与外部传播参数之间的依赖性，消除了弱可识别性导致的特征性脊状结构，从而提高了参数的可识别性。

Conclusion: 图谱解析的家庭模型（graph-resolved household models）能够在保持解析与计算可处理性的前提下，实现改进的传播推断，为传染病传播参数识别提供了新的途径。

Abstract: Models incorporating household structure, with different rates of transmission within and between households, are widely used in infectious disease epidemiology. These models can be calibrated using final-size data in which transmission ordering is ignored because it does not affect the distribution of final outbreak sizes. In particular, many distinct transmission histories produce identical final epidemiological outcomes, making it difficult to distinguish internal (within-household) from external (between-household) transmission and limiting parameter identifiability. Here, we develop a continuous-time Markov chain formulation for household transmission dynamics in which the model state space is expanded to include transmission graphs describing infection direction and order, with idealised pathogen genomic data used to identify the transmission histories compatible with observations. We conduct simulation studies which show that incorporating genetic information substantially concentrates the regions of high likelihood compared with models based on epidemiological data alone. In particular, genomic data reduces the dependence between internal and external transmission parameters, removing the characteristic ridge associated with their weak identifiability. These results demonstrate that graph-resolved household models enable improved transmission inference while maintaining analytical and computational tractability.

</details>

### [3] [The Landau-Dirac operator with shell interactions: self-adjointness and clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20665)
*Badreddine Benhellal,Vincent Bruneau,Pablo Miranda*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究二维Dirac算子受恒定磁场及光滑闭合曲线上可变系数静电场与Lorentz标量delta相互作用扰动后的谱性质，在非临界情形下证明了离散谱在Landau-Dirac能级处按曲线对数容量的速率聚集，并发现了聚集侧随临界值位置而变化的意外现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解二维Dirac算子与delta型相互作用结合时的谱结构，特别是非临界与临界情形下的本质谱与离散谱行为，揭示边界几何（对数容量）对特征值聚集速率的影响。

Method: 利用边界积分算子的拟微分性质分析Dirac算子自伴性，分别处理非临界和临界情形，通过共形耦合构造外部边值问题的特征值簇，将无限质量边界条件作为特例处理。

Result: 非临界情形下本质谱保持为Landau-Dirac能级不变；临界情形下谱隙中出现新的本质谱区间。非临界情形下特征值在各级Landau-Dirac能级处按曲线对数容量所决定的速率聚集，且聚集侧（上方或下方）随参数相对于临界值的位置发生反转。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了delta相互作用扰动下二维Dirac算子的精细谱结构，特别是聚集侧反转这一新颖现象，为理解量子系统在磁场与奇异势阱相互作用下的离散谱行为提供了理论框架。

Abstract: We consider the two-dimensional Dirac operator with constant magnetic field that is perturbed by a combination of electrostatic and Lorentz-scalar delta interactions with variable coefficients supported on a smooth closed curve. Self-adjointness is studied in the so called non critical and critical cases. In the non-critical case the essential spectrum is unchanged - it remains to be the set of the Landau-Dirac levels, the eigenvalues of infinite multiplicity of the unperturbed operator - while in the critical case an additional interval of essential spectrum emerges in the spectral gap containing zero. Our main result concerns the discrete spectrum in the non-critical case: using the pseudodifferential properties of the involved boundary integral operators, we show that the eigenvalues accumulate at each Landau-Dirac level at a rate governed by the logarithmic capacity of the curve. A novel and surprising phenomenon is the change in the side of the accumulation depending on the position relative to the critical value. As a byproduct, clusters of eigenvalues for a family of exterior boundary value problems are obtained via confining couplings; the infinite-mass boundary condition arises as a special case.

</details>


### [4] [On quantum channels: extreme points, topology, and categorical properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20689)
*J. M. S. T. da Silva*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文从数学角度系统研究了量子信道（CPTP映射和酉CPTP映射/UCPTP映射），构建了各种秩的极端映射，探讨了张量积的极端性保持问题，对半笛卡尔范畴中的二元积和余积进行了分类，并通过CW复形等拓扑工具研究了极端点集闭包的拓扑结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信道是量子信息论的核心对象，其数学结构（特别是极端点的分类与几何构型）对理解量子操作的基本限制具有重要意义。已有的研究尚未完整分类CPTP和UCPTP映射的极端点，且对该集合的拓扑结构缺乏深入认识。

Method: 采用纯数学方法：利用凸集的极端点理论构造特定秩的极端CPTP和UCPTP映射；通过张量积分析研究极端性的保持与破坏；使用范畴论中的半笛卡尔结构分类二元积与余积；借助CW复形理论和拓扑不变量分析极端点集闭包的空间结构。

Result: 成功构造了每个可能秩的极端CPTP映射，以及维度2的极端UCPTP映射；对秩≥3的UCPTP极端映射在多个维度与秩下均有构造。证明了极端CPTP映射的张量积仍为极端，但UCPTP映射的张量积极端性不恒成立并给出大量反例。对二元积证明了仅在平凡情形存在，对二元余积求得了存在时所需的维度。在拓扑方面，获得了极端CPTP点集闭包的部分分解，该分解类似复射影空间的CW结构。

Conclusion: 本文全面刻画了CPTP与UCPTP量子信道的极端点结构及其张量积行为，给出了范畴论中积与余积的分类结果，并揭示了极端点集闭包与复射影空间之间的拓扑联系，为量子信道理论的数学基础提供了重要补充。

Abstract: In this thesis, quantum channels are studied from the point of view of Mathematics. We studied the CPTP (completely positive trace preserving) and UCPTP channels, the latter being the unital channels. In both cases, the sets are compact convex, so they are the closure of their extreme points. We constructed extreme CPTP maps for every possible rank. We also constructed extreme UCPTP maps with rank 2 for each possible dimension. For ranks of at least 3, we constructed extreme UCPTP maps for many dimensions and ranks. We also proved that the tensor product of extreme CPTP maps is an extreme CPTP map. For UCPTP maps, we proved that the same is not always true, with many counterexamples. It is known that the category of CPTP maps is semicartesian. We add to this a classification of binary products. We proved that products only exist in trivial cases. For binary coproducts, we obtained the dimensions they should have, if they exist. In both cases, topological techniques were used. For this reason, we also investigated the topology of the closure of the set of extreme points of the CPTP maps. Topological invariants can be computed from decompositions of a space such as CW structures. These are a type of decomposition in which the space is expressed as a gluing of balls. In the search for a CW structure for the closure of the set of extreme CPTP maps, we obtained a partial decomposition that resembles a CW structure of the complex projective space.

</details>


### [5] [Bethe-root configurations and spectral degeneracy in the open XXZ chain with degenerate boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20815)
*Shizhang Wu,Shu Chen,Junpeng Cao,Xin Zhang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有简并开边界条件的自旋1/2 XXZ链中Bethe根构型的结构，在完全简并情形下将物理解分为三类，在部分简并情形下分为两类，并提出各类物理解组的计数公式。同时观察到完全简并情形下的谱简并现象，并通过Bethe根中的特定幻影弦（phantom strings）加以解释，还讨论了对角极限下的分类与谱简并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 简并开边界条件会对Bethe Ansatz方程的物理解结构产生重要影响，理解这些解的类别与计数对于认识开边界自旋链的谱结构、简并性及其物理机制具有重要意义。

Method: 通过对简并开边界条件下XXZ链的Bethe Ansatz方程进行系统分析，将物理解按边界参数的简并程度进行分类；分别给出完全简并和部分简并情形下各类物理解组的计数公式；通过分析Bethe根中幻影弦的存在来揭示谱简并的根源。

Result: 在完全简并情形下，物理解被分类为三种类型；在部分简并情形下被分类为两种类型。给出了每种类型的物理解组计数公式，并观察到完全简并情形下的谱简并现象，证明了这些谱简并可自然归因于Bethe根中特定幻影弦的存在。

Conclusion: 通过Bethe根构型的分类和幻影弦分析，完整刻画了简并开边界XXZ链的物理解结构，并为谱简并现象提供了清晰的物理解释；该分类及谱简并在对角极限下仍然成立。

Abstract: We investigate the structure of Bethe-root configurations in the spin-1/2 XXZ chain with degenerate open boundaries. The physical solutions of the Bethe Ansatz equations are classified into three types in the fully degenerate case and two types in the partially degenerate case, for which we propose counting formulas for the number of physical solution sets of each type. Furthermore, we observe spectral degeneracies in the fully degenerate case and show that they can be naturally explained by the presence of specific phantom strings in the Bethe roots. The classification and resulting spectral degeneracies in the diagonal limit are also discussed.

</details>


### [6] [Axiomatization of the Levin--Wen Wave Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21109)
*Zhengwei Liu,Zishuo Zhao*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了六个公理来内在刻画Levin-Wen模型的基态波函数，无需预先假设范畴对称数据或哈密顿量，从而解决了该领域的长期开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Levin-Wen模型提供了与酉融合范畴相关的拓扑序的格点实现，但长期以来缺少对Levin-Wen基态波函数的内在刻画——即不依赖先验的范畴对称数据或哈密顿量的特征化方法。

Method: 提出六条针对多尺度格点上波函数族的公理，通过这些公理重建底层酉融合范畴，并证明所得波函数映射到涌现范畴的非零Levin-Wen基态矢量。

Result: 六条公理足以从波函数本身重建出底层酉融合范畴，且证明重建后的波函数对应涌现范畴的非零Levin-Wen基态矢量。

Conclusion: 该工作首次提供了Levin-Wen基态波函数的纯内在（intrinsic）公理化刻画，解决了长期开放问题，为拓扑序的格点波函数表征奠定了新的理论基础。

Abstract: The Levin--Wen model provides a lattice realization of topological orders associated with a given unitary fusion category. A longstanding open problem is to characterize Levin--Wen ground-state wave functions intrinsically, without assuming a priori categorical symmetry data or a Hamiltonian. We address this by proposing six axioms on a family of wave functions defined on lattices at multiple scales. These axioms allow us to reconstruct the underlying unitary fusion category and prove that the resulting wave functions map to nonzero Levin--Wen ground-state vectors of the emergent category.

</details>


### [7] [The transverse matter Hamiltonian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21211)
*Andrea Marini,Kai Wu,Riccardo Reho*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文扩展了Fermi在1932年提出的经典Gauss方程方法，通过量子规范变换将横向矢势替换为流-流和流-流-密度相互作用，在费米子空间中得到一个纯物质、完全不相干且规范不变的哈密顿量，该哈密顿量避免了Haag定理导致的微扰论失效，并为声子等元激发的电磁纵-横劈裂提供了量子起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fermi在1932年利用经典Gauss方程从纵向电磁势导出了Coulomb密度-密度相互作用，但这一程序从未被扩展到横向矢势分量。此外，Haag定理指出在含相互作用的量子场论中微扰论可能失效，需要一种方法去除相干性以恢复可计算性。

Method: 首先，将Fermi经典程序推广到横向矢势分量；其次，利用全量子规范变换（canonical transformation）将横向矢势替换为流-流和流-流-密度相互作用；然后，将变换后的哈密顿量投影到费米子空间，得到一个纯物质、不相干且规范不变的哈密顿量。

Result: 得到了一个纯物质、完全不相干且规范不变的哈密顿量。去除相干性避免了Haag定理所预言的微扰论失效，使得在变换空间中能够引入图论方法。该横向哈密顿量为声子和其他元激发的电磁纵-横劈裂提供了量子起源。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅将Fermi程序从纵向扩展到横向，还揭示了对现有基于Fermi程序的电流理论的深远影响，并为理解元激发中的纵-横电磁劈裂提供了新的量子框架。

Abstract: Enrico Fermi in 1932 used classical Gauss equation to derive the Coulomb density--density interaction from the longitudinal electromagnetic potential. In this work we extend the Fermi procedure to the transverse component of the vector potential. By using a fully quantum canonical transformation, we replace the transverse vector potential with a current--current and current--current--density interactions. The transformed Hamiltonian is, then, projected in the fermionic space providing a matter--only, totally incoherent and gauge respecting Hamiltonian. The removal of coherence will avoid the breakdown of perturbation theory predicted by Haag's theorem, and make possible to introduce, in the transformed space, the diagrammatic approach. After discussing the implications of the transverse interactions on the current theories based on the Fermi procedure, we conclude by showing how the transverse Hamiltonian provides the quantum origin of the electromagnetic longitudinal--transverse splitting of phonons and other elemental excitations.

</details>


### [8] [Spectral gaps for mean-field Coulomb gases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21215)
*Simon Becker,Angeliki Menegaki*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了一类排斥性（单组分）库仑气体在维度d≥3上的Poincaré不等式，且该不等式关于粒子数一致成立。允许逆温度β_N依赖于N，并在以有效相互作用强度Θ_N表示的弱耦合条件下，得到相应过阻尼Langevin动力学的指数松弛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 库仑气体是统计物理和数学物理中的重要模型，其大粒子数极限下的动力学性质（如指数松弛）具有根本意义。已有的Poincaré不等式结果通常依赖强耦合或固定粒子数，需要建立对粒子数一致成立的下界，以理解热化时间尺度和动力学临界行为。

Method: 核心工具是单粒子位上的Poincaré不等式，该不等式对库仑极点的数量和位置一致成立，包括重合极点情形。通过moving-pole估计和Dobrushin-Wu张量化方法，将单粒子估计提升为多粒子系统的下界；同时利用质心坐标获得匹配的上界，从而得到完整的Poincaré常数界。

Result: 在弱耦合条件Θ_N充分小的假设下，证明了库仑气体谱隙（Poincaré常数倒数）关于粒子数N一致有界，进而得到过阻尼Langevin动力学的指数收敛速率；上界与下界通过质心坐标分析表明所得界是最优的。

Conclusion: 该结果将强耦合库仑气体的谱隙理论推广到温度可依赖粒子数的弱耦合情形，为理解高维库仑系统的动力学混合行为提供了统一框架，并开辟了处理重合极点等奇异构型的张量化分析方法。

Abstract: We prove a Poincaré inequality, uniform in the particle number, for repulsive (one-component) Coulomb gases in dimensions $d\ge3$, with inverse temperature $β_N$ that is allowed to depend on $N$. We assume a weak-coupling condition expressed in terms of the effective interaction strength $Θ_N$ seen by a single particle. This yields exponential relaxation for the associated overdamped Langevin dynamics. The main ingredient is a one-site Poincaré inequality uniform in the number and positions of the Coulomb poles, including coincident poles. A moving-pole estimate and Dobrushin-Wu tensorization yield the many-particle lower bound, while the center-of-mass coordinate gives the matching upper bound.

</details>


### [9] [The spin-1/2 Heisenberg XXZ chain and the Lorentz mirror model with loop weight 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21306)
*Kieran Ryan*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了自旋1/2 Heisenberg XXZ链在Δ∈[-1,1/2]范围内，长度为L的环面上的基态收敛到无限体积基态，且自旋-自旋关联函数S_0^(1)S_x^(1)呈多项式衰减。在Δ∈[-1,0]范围内，获得了更强结果：有限体积有限温度态的收敛、L和β=1/T极限可交换、动态关联函数上的收敛以及时间依赖关联函数非指数衰减的多项式衰减。方法上不依赖Bethe Ansatz，而是通过Lorentz镜面模型与six-vertex模型之间的耦合（其中一个耦合是新的），利用six-vertex模型高度函数的离域性来证明关联函数的收敛与多项式衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来，XXZ链的基态收敛和关联函数衰减性质主要依赖Bethe Ansatz方法，但这种方法在动态关联函数、有限温度态以及极限交换性等方面存在局限。作者旨在发展一种不依赖Bethe Ansatz的新方法，通过概率模型（Lorentz镜面模型和six-vertex模型）来严格证明更广泛的收敛性和衰减结果，填补这些方向的空白。

Method: 作者采用如下方法：(1) 将XXZ模型的loop表示与Lorentz镜面模型的连接概率联系起来；(2) 建立Lorentz镜面模型与six-vertex模型的两个耦合（其中一个为新耦合），利用six-vertex模型高度函数的离域性（该结果由多位作者证明）来推导连接概率趋于0；(3) 在对称情形下证明连接概率的多项式衰减；(4) 进一步证明six-vertex模型的空间-时间版本中高度函数离域，并通过类似耦合证明Ueltschi引入的XXZ模型loop表示中连接概率收敛到0。

Result: (1) 在Δ∈[-1,1/2]范围内，证明了环面上基态收敛到无限体积基态，且自旋-自旋关联函数多项式衰减；(2) 在Δ∈[-1,0]范围内，证明了有限体积有限温度态的收敛、L和β可任意顺序取极限、动态（Euclidean）关联函数上的收敛，以及时间依赖关联函数S_0^(1)S_x^(1)(t)随|(x,t)|衰减到零且不能指数快衰减；(3) 在Lorentz镜面模型（loop weight 2）的大范围参数下，证明了连接概率趋于0且对称情形下多项式衰减；(4) 证明了six-vertex模型时空版本中高度函数的离域性。

Conclusion: 本文的关键贡献在于：(a) 首次严格证明了动态关联函数上的收敛性以及L和β极限的可交换性；(b) 除Δ=0,-1两个特殊点外，关联函数多项式衰减结果在严格层面上是新的；(c) 方法论上是全新的，完全不依赖Bethe Ansatz，仅通过概率模型耦合方法得以实现，开辟了研究量子自旋链关联性质的新途径。

Abstract: We prove that for the spin-1/2 Heisenberg XXZ chain in the range $Δ\in[-1,1/2]$, the ground state on the torus of length $L$ converges to an infinite volume ground state $\langle\cdot\rangle$ as $L\to\infty$, and that the spin-spin correlation $\langle S_0^{(1)}S_x^{(1)}\rangle$ decays polynomially fast in $x$. In the range $Δ\in[-1,0]$ we have the stronger results: that the convergence holds for several finite-volume, finite temperature states, that $L$ and $β=1/T$ can be taken to infinity in any order, that the convergence holds on (Euclidean) dynamic correlators, and that $\langle S_0^{(1)}S_x^{(1)}(t)\rangle$ decays to zero in $|(x,t)|$, and cannot decay exponentially fast.
  The convergence of the ground state in infinite volume is known rigorously by Bethe Ansatz methods, while our correlation decay results (apart from the points $Δ=0,-1$), the convergence on dynamic correlators and the interchangeability of limits are new at the rigorous level. Moreover our methods are new and do not use any Bethe Ansatz techniques, using only the following related probabilistic models.
  In the Lorentz mirror model with loop weight 2, a model of random loops on the square lattice, in a large range of parameters we prove that connection probabilities tend to 0, and do so polynomially fast in the symmetric case of the model. Our proof uses two couplings of this mirror model with the six-vertex model, one of which is new. The main input is the delocalisation of the height function of the six-vertex model proved by several authors.
  We further prove that the height function of a certain space-time version of the six-vertex model delocalises and then prove through analogous couplings to those mentioned above that connection probabilities converge to 0 in the loop representation of the XXZ model introduced by Ueltschi.

</details>

### [10] [Strong growth and Goldstein subgradients in piecewise smooth optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20642)
*Adrian S. Lewis,Fahaar M. Pirani*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文探讨了Lipschitz函数在局部极小值点附近的一种新的增长条件（该条件最近在Goldstein次梯度算法背景下被提出），研究了其在分段二次连续可微目标函数上的成立条件，并验证了该条件在算法线性收敛分析中的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Goldstein次梯度基算法在实际中常被观察到近似线性收敛，但缺乏严格的理论解释。作者旨在澄清这种新增长条件在什么情况下对分段二次连续可微目标函数成立，从而为算法的线性收敛提供理论支撑。

Method: 作者将目标函数限定为分段二次连续可微（piecewise twice continuously differentiable）。在凸情形下，证明当目标函数额外满足强凸性时，新增长条件成立；在非凸情形下，证明二次增长结合活跃梯度的正则性性质（regularity property for active gradients）足以保证增长条件成立。并通过数值实验验证假设的重要性。

Result: 对于分段二次连续可微函数：(1) 若目标函数同时强凸，则新增长条件成立；(2) 非凸情形下，二次增长加上活跃梯度的正则性足以推导出该增长条件。计算实验展示了各假设条件的关键作用。

Conclusion: 该新增长条件在分段光滑函数类中具有明确的可验证条件（强凸性或二次增长+活跃梯度正则性），为解释Goldstein次梯度算法在实际中的近似线性收敛行为提供了理论基础，同时实验表明假设条件不可随意放宽。

Abstract: We explore a new growth condition for Lipschitz functions around local minimizers, recently proposed in the context of Goldstein-subgradient-based algorithms and their behavior in practice. On generic examples, these algorithms are often observed to converge approximately linearly. We focus on objectives that are piecewise twice continuously differentiable. In that case, the new growth condition holds when the objective is, in addition, strongly convex. In the nonconvex case, we prove that quadratic growth in conjunction with a regularity property for the active gradients suffices. Computational experiments illustrate the importance of the assumptions.

</details>


### [11] [ResiliFlow: An Open Transport World Model for Infrastructure Perception and Disaster Resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20709)
*Junxiang Xu,Vinayak Dixit,S. Travis Waller,Divya Jayakumar Nair,Qianwen,Guo,Sisi Jian,Xiao Wen,Ashutosh Ashutosh,Sunhyung Yoo,Julius Secadiningrat,Jingni Guo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: ResiliFlow是一个开放的交通世界模型平台，将交通韧性分析工作流从碎片化的脚本、模型和报告整合为两个关联工作区：灾害交通韧性分析（六项地图功能）和AI交通基础设施感知与决策支持（街景与卫星证据识别）。该平台采用八步闭环循环（感知、预测、模型开发、验证、执行、决策、反馈、记忆），并配备本地助手和可选多供应商LLM Copilot将用户问题转化为对可执行工具的受限调用。研究验证功能记录假设与检查，实验表明在八个可见条件类别上实现了准确识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通韧性研究相关工作通常分散在数据预处理脚本、网络模型、仿真工具、图像检查系统和报告中，这种碎片化使得从观测到可测试、可审查决策的转化变得困难，缺乏一个整合的、可检查、可复用、以问题为导向的系统。

Method: 本文提出并实现了ResiliFlow开放交通世界模型平台，架构上连接两个工作区：1）灾害交通韧性分析工作区，提供关键道路/关键区域识别、恢复优先级排序、中断路由、韧性测试和情景仿真等六项地图中心功能；2）AI交通基础设施感知与决策支持工作区，组织街景和卫星证据，检测可见的道路、人行道和路缘条件，并准备人类审查的干预规划。两工作区共享八步循环；研究验证记录假设与检查；本地Assistant和可选的多供应商LLM Copilot将用户问题转换为受限的可执行工具调用。

Result: 实验中在八个可见条件类别上实现了准确识别，并通过紧凑的误差分析展示了困难案例如何指导持续学习。文档化内容包括平台架构、代表性数学模型、界面证据和计算机视觉学习结果。

Conclusion: ResiliFlow展示了交通模型可以成为一个可检查、可复用、以问题为导向的系统，而非分散分析的集合。系统发布旨在支持研究协作、公众审查和在机构审查下的扩展。

Abstract: Transport resilience work is often split across separate data preparation scripts, network models, simulation tools, image inspection systems and reports. This fragmentation makes it difficult to move from an observation to a tested and reviewable decision. We introduce ResiliFlow, an open transport world model concept and an implemented platform for infrastructure resilience, response and recovery. The platform connects two workspaces. Disaster Transport Resilience Analysis provides six map-centred functions for critical-road and critical-area identification, recovery prioritisation, disruption routing, resilience testing and scenario simulation. AI-based Transport Infrastructure Perception and Decision Support organises street-level and satellite evidence, detects visible road, footpath and kerb conditions, and prepares these observations for human-reviewed intervention planning. Both workspaces share an eight-step cycle of perception, prediction, model development, verification, execution, decision, feedback and memory. Research Validation records assumptions and checks, while a local Assistant and an optional multi-provider large language model Copilot translate user questions into bounded calls to executable tools. We document the platform architecture, representative mathematical models, interface evidence and computer-vision learning results. Examples show accurate recognition across eight visible-condition classes, while compact error analysis demonstrates how difficult cases guide continued learning. ResiliFlow shows how transport models can become an inspectable, reusable and question-led system rather than a collection of disconnected analyses. The accompanying release is intended to support research collaboration, public scrutiny and extension under institutional review.

</details>

*Shuaicheng Tong,Pascal Van Hentenryck*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于同伦延拓法的离散控制电压/无功优化（VVO）方法，用于协调OLTC和电容器组等开关设备，在实用时间内生成AC可行的调度点，同时降低电压偏差、无功调度量和发电成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 输电系统运营商在将市场出清的DC调度与AC物理特性调和后，得到的设定点仍可能违反电压和无功限制。维护安全电压剖面和充足的无功支撑需要可在运行中实施的快速校正决策，即需要一个高效的离散控制VVO方法。

Method: 提出一种基于同伦（homotopy）的延拓法用于离散控制VVO，该方法协同优化OLTC和电容器组等开关设备，在调度过程中将离散控制变量纳入优化框架。

Result: 在IEEE、PEGASE和RTE系统上的实验表明，该方法能在实用时间内生成AC可行的设定点，同时减少电压偏差、无功调度量和发电成本。使用ACPF调整后的DC调度作为输入时，与使用AC可行调度相比性能相当。

Conclusion: 所提出的VVO方法能够自然地集成到现有输电调度实践中，强化电网运行的安全性和经济性，生成的设定点既满足AC可行性要求又具有实际可实施性。

Abstract: Transmission system operators often reconcile market-cleared DC dispatches with AC physics through power-flow solves (ACPF), yet the resulting setpoints can still violate voltage (Volt) and reactive-power (VAR) limits. Maintaining secure voltage profiles and adequate VAR support therefore requires fast corrective decisions that are implementable in operation. This paper presents a novel homotopy-based continuation method for discrete-control Volt/VAR Optimization (VVO) that coordinates switchable devices, such as on-load tap-changing transformers (OLTCs) and capacitor banks (CBs). Experiments on IEEE, PEGASE, and RTE systems show that the proposed VVO produces AC-feasible setpoints within practical runtime, while reducing voltage deviation, VAR dispatch, and generation cost. VVO also achieves comparable performance using ACPF-adjusted DC dispatches as inputs relative to AC-feasible dispatches, indicating that it can be integrated naturally into existing transmission dispatch practices to strengthen grid operation.

</details>


### [13] [Time-Inconsistent MDPs with Entropy Regularization: Equilibrium Existence and Policy Iteration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20811)
*Fengyuan Cao,Zhenhua Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有可数无限状态空间和无界奖励函数的无限时域时间不一致马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），通过熵正则化与加权泛函分析方法建立松弛反馈均衡的存在性，并设计策略迭代算法（PIA）求解正则化均衡，最终通过软最大近似将迭代策略转化为原问题加权的ε-均衡并给出明确的遗憾估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间不一致的MDP（如奖励显式依赖于初始时间和初始状态）在经济学和运筹学中广泛存在，但现有理论多在有限状态或有界奖励下处理。可数无限状态空间与无界奖励函数给均衡的存在性证明和数值求解带来根本性困难，亟需一套统一的泛函分析框架和可证的算法收敛理论。

Method: (1) 引入熵正则化将原问题转化为正则化问题，通过不动点算子刻画正则松弛均衡；(2) 引入两个具有不同职能的权重函数——一个控制奖励和值函数的增长，另一个定义环境的加权空间——构造紧不变集，在乘积拓扑下借助Schauder-Tychonoff不动点定理证明正则化均衡的存在性，且不变集对λ∈(0,1]可一致选取；(3) 令λ→0+，利用紧性、Gibbs策略的浓度性质以及一致可积性论证，证明子序列极限是原无正则化问题的松弛均衡；(4) 在加权折扣结构下，设计PIA算法，在充分强折扣条件下建立指数收敛和正则化均衡的唯一性；(5) 将策略迭代误差与定量的软最大近似界相结合，证明迭代策略构成原问题的加权ε-均衡并给出显式遗憾界。

Result: 建立了正则化松弛均衡的存在性（通过紧不变集和Schauder-Tychonoff定理），并证明了当正则化参数趋于零时子序列极限收敛到原问题的松弛均衡。在充分强的折扣条件下，PIA算法具有指数收敛速率且正则化均衡唯一，迭代策略构成原问题的加权ε-均衡，并获得了显式遗憾估计。提供了强折扣下PIA收敛的数值示例和弱折扣下算法失效的反例。

Conclusion: 本文为时间不一致、可数无限状态、无界奖励的MDP提供了完整的理论框架：一方面通过熵正则化和加权泛函分析严格建立了均衡存在性；另一方面，在强折扣条件下PIA算法不仅指数收敛，还能直接给出原问题的可量化近似解。弱折扣下PIA失效的反例揭示了正则化方法的适用范围和局限性，为未来研究提供了重要边界。

Abstract: We study infinite-horizon time-inconsistent Markov decision processes with a countably infinite state space and unbounded reward functions. The reward is allowed to depend explicitly on the initial time and initial state, thereby accommodating general sources of time inconsistency. We seek relaxed feedback equilibria, and our approach is based on entropy regularization and weighted functional analytic methods. With entropy regularization, we characterize a regular relaxed equilibrium through a fixed-point operator. By introducing two weight functions with distinct roles, one controlling the growth of rewards and values and the other defining the ambient weighted space, we construct a compact invariant set under a product topology and apply the Schauder-Tychonoff fixed-point theorem to establish existence of regularized equilibria. Importantly, the invariant set can be chosen uniformly for small entropy weight $λ\in(0,1]$. We then let $λ\to0+$ and show, through compactness, concentration of Gibbs policies, and uniform-integrability arguments, that a subsequential limit is a relaxed equilibrium of the original unregularized problem.
  We further study a policy iteration algorithm (PIA) for the entropy-regularized equilibrium problem. Under a weighted-discounting structure and sufficiently strong discounting, we establish exponential convergence and uniqueness of the regularized equilibrium in a suitable weighted Banach space. Combining the policy-iteration error with a quantitative soft-max approximation bound, we show that the iterated policies constitute weighted $\varepsilon$-equilibria for the original unregularized problem and derive an explicit regret estimate. A numerical example illustrating the convergence of PIA under strong discounting and a counterexample demonstrating its failure under weak discounting are also provided.

</details>


### [14] [Failure of Whole-Sequence Convergence in Variable-Smoothing Full-Splitting Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20859)
*Min Tao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了结构化非凸非光滑分数规划中基于平滑的全分裂近端次梯度方法（S-FSPS）的整序列收敛性问题。作者构造了两个反例，表明即使每个聚点都是精确极限提升驻点，生成的原始序列也可能具有无限长度轨迹且聚点集为{1}×S¹，从而否定地回答了整序列必须收敛的猜想。这是首次显式构造表明不可和淡平滑不能保证变平滑全分裂格式的整序列收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于S-FSPS方法的收敛理论仅建立了子序列收敛到精确极限提升驻点的性质，但整个生成序列是否必须收敛这一问题悬而未决。该问题对理解该类算法的理论性质具有基础性意义，直接关系到算法的实际可靠性。

Method: 通过构造两个"可接受的"（admissible）具体实例来给出反例。第一个构造中线性算子A非零且每个生成的对偶迭代非零；第二个构造中可行集为全维、A具有满行秩，且f∘K和g∘A在可行集上均非恒定。通过分析两个实例中序列的聚点集与轨迹长度，证明整序列不收敛但所有聚点均为精确极限提升驻点。

Result: 两个构造实例均显示：算法的原始序列聚点集为{1}×S¹（单位圆），轨迹长度无限，但每个聚点都是精确极限提升驻点。第一个构造还导出了相应的变平滑单循环全分裂方法（用于非凸非光滑复合优化）的非收敛例子，其中仅子序列收敛到精确极限驻点。

Conclusion: 整序列收敛对于S-FSPS方法不成立。即使使用不可和淡平滑并保证精确极限驻点性，也并不能推出变平滑全分裂方案的整序列收敛。这是该方向的首次显式构造性反例，为后续研究更严格收敛条件的必要性与可能性指明了方向。

Abstract: We investigate whole-sequence convergence of the smoothing-based full-splitting proximal subgradient method (S-FSPS) for structured nonconvex and nonsmooth fractional programs, introduced as Algorithm~4.1 by Boţ, Li, and Tao (\emph{SIAM J. Optim.}, 35(4):2623--2653, 2025). The existing convergence theory establishes only the existence of subsequences converging to exact limiting lifted stationary points, leaving open whether the entire generated sequence must converge. We answer this question in the negative by constructing two admissible instances whose primal sequences have cluster set \(\{1\}\times\mathbb S^1\) and infinite-length trajectories, although every cluster point is an exact limiting lifted stationary point. In the first construction, the linear operator \(A\) is nonzero and every generated dual iterate is nonzero. In the second, the feasible set is full-dimensional, \(A\) has full row rank, and both \(f\circ K\) and \(g\circ A\) are nonconstant on the feasible set. The first construction also yields a nonconvergent example for the corresponding variable-smoothing, single-loop, full-splitting method for nonconvex and nonsmooth composite optimization, although a subsequence converges to an exact limiting stationary point. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first explicit construction showing that vanishing nonsummable smoothing, even together with exact limiting stationarity, does not imply whole-sequence convergence for variable-smoothing full-splitting schemes.

</details>


### [15] [Maximum Covering Network Design on Graphs with Low Connectivity: Dynamic Programming and Block-Cut Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20894)
*Felix Rauh,Jannik Matuschke,Hande Yaman*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究最大覆盖网络设计问题（MCNDP），即在设施选址和网络升级之间共享单一预算，最大化目标出行距离内的覆盖人口。作者提出一种精确的动态规划框架，利用道路网络低连通性（存在大量割点）的特性：在树上可通过简单的递归快速求解；在一般低连通网络上通过割点分解并嵌入MILP求解各子问题。实验表明该框架在306个测试案例中超过80%的实例优于或等同于直接求解MILP，且单次运行即可获得完整覆盖率-预算曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统设施选址模型将网络视为固定且预算给定，但实际规划中预算需同时在设施建设和路网升级之间分配，且决策者希望看到覆盖率随预算增长的完整曲线而非单一方案。该问题即使在最简单的网络上也是困难的，需要高效的精确求解方法。

Method: 利用真实道路网络低连通性（存在多个割点）的特性，设计了精确的动态规划框架。在树形网络上，递归自包含，每个状态简化为少数设施与升级选择，无需求解器即可快速计算；在一般低连通网络上，框架在割点处分解问题，嵌入给定MILP求解各子问题，并通过界面处的覆盖条件协调各子问题。该框架在单次运行中评估所有预算水平，同时获得完整覆盖率-预算曲线。

Result: 在306个测试案例中，该框架在超过80%的实例上匹配或优于直接求解MILP；在较大预算和出行距离下，树形网络上的递归方法性能优于直接求解MILP；完整覆盖率-预算曲线可在单次运行中无额外成本获得，而直接求解需将时间分配给各个预算水平。

Conclusion: 提出了一种利用网络低连通性结构的精确动态规划框架，既能高效求解最大覆盖网络设计问题，又能一次性生成完整的覆盖率-预算曲线，为公共设施规划提供了有价值的决策支持工具。

Abstract: Planning accessible public services such as health care, emergency response, and schools often requires not only choosing where to open facilities but also improving the network that connects people to them, for example upgrading flood-prone roads in vulnerable regions. Most location models, however, take the network as fixed and the budget as given. We study the Maximum Covering Network Design Problem, in which a single budget is shared between opening facilities and upgrading weak links to maximize the population within a target travel distance of an open facility. The problem is hard even on the simplest networks, and planners usually want to see how coverage grows with the budget, not a single plan.

On trees, the recursion is self-contained: each state reduces to a few simple facility and upgrade choices that are fast to compute without a solver, giving predictable running times; for larger budgets and travel distances it outperforms solving the MILP formulation directly. On general low-connectivity networks, the framework decomposes the problem at the cut points and embeds a given MILP formulation to solve the resulting pieces, coordinating them through coverage conditions at the interfaces. This lets us compare a formulation on its own against the same formulation inside the framework: across 306 test cases the framework matches or outperforms direct solving on more than 80% of instances. Because it evaluates all budget levels in a single run, it also yields the full coverage-versus-budget curve at no extra cost, whereas direct solving must split its time across individual budgets.

Method: 提出ARDLS优化模型，将已确立且具有唯一解的优先化算子（如归一化技术、特征向量法、奇异值分解、余弦最大化、伪逆Gram矩阵——即加权最小二乘的闭式解）作为理论锚点，引入正则化惩罚项中。通过系统性地打破优化景观中的数学对称性，使优化景观倾斜，从而保证收敛到唯一全局最小值。

Result: 综合数值实验和仿真验证表明，ARDLS框架在保证严格数学唯一性的前提下，成功降低了与既有优先权算子之间的均方根误差。

Abstract: Pairwise reciprocal matrices are fundamental to the Analytic Hierarchy Process (AHP), a decision-making model. While the Direct Least Squares (DLS) method provides an intuitive mechanism for deriving priority vectors without complex transformations, the DLS provides multiple solutions. Under high levels of inconsistency, such as cyclic contradictions, this non-convexity yields multiple distinct global minima, resulting in unstable priority rankings that critically depend on initial algorithmic guesses. To overcome this structural deficiency, this paper introduces the Anchored Regularized Direct Least Squares (ARDLS) optimization model. ARDLS integrates uniquely determined established prioritization operators, such as normalization techniques, the Eigenvector method, Singular Value Decomposition, Cosine Maximization, and the Pseudo-Inverse Gram Matrix (the closed-form solution of Weighted Least Squares), as theoretical anchors within a regularization penalty. This integration systematically breaks mathematical symmetries, tilting the optimization landscape to guarantee convergence upon a single, unique global minimum. Comprehensive numerical experiments and simulations validate that the ARDLS framework successfully reduces root mean square error among established priority operators, while guaranteeing strict mathematical uniqueness. The proposed ARDLS may be the ideal alternative for the AHP applied to many application domains.

</details>

Abstract: We develop a new direct accelerated Newton method for minimizing convex functions with Lipschitz continuous Hessian. The algorithm uses only primal variables and performs just one linear solve per iteration. With a simple predetermined choice of parameters, it achieves the global convergence rate of $O(1/k^3)$ in terms of the functional residual. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first second-order method for this problem class attaining this rate while relying solely on one linear system solve per iteration (without solving auxiliary nonlinear regularized subproblems, such as cubic regularization, performing nonlinear parameter searches, or using dual extragradient corrections). Our method can be implemented in a Hessian-free way, using an inexact linear system solver, while preserving the fast global rate. We further extend our construction to arbitrary geometry through Bregman divergence, and to composite optimization problems.

Abstract: Let $G$ be a complex reductive group and $C_m(G)_1$ the identity component of the space of $m$-tuples of commuting elements in $G$. Abstract: We give negative answers to two tilting-completion questions for finite-dimensional algebras. We construct two finite-dimensional basic connected quasi-hereditary $\mathbb C$-algebras. The full-rank example also yields counterexamples to two conjectures: Enomoto's Self-orthogonal Wakamatsu-tilting Conjecture and the Self-orthogonal Faithful Conjecture of Chen, Li, Zhang, and Zhao. We further show that the Self-orthogonal Wakamatsu-tilting Conjecture holds for all finite-dimensional algebras if and only if the Self-orthogonal Faithful Conjecture holds for all finite-dimensional algebras.

Abstract: This paper studies the numerical solution of elastic wave propagation on networks, modeled by elastodynamic equations posed on each edge, coupled at the nodes through suitable transmission conditions. We propose and analyze a hybridizable discontinuous Galerkin method that exploits the network structure to reduce the global problem at each time step to a linear system whose size depends only on the number of network nodes and not on the polynomial degree of the discretization. Combining it with an energy-conservative implicit time discretization, we derive a priori error estimates of optimal order in space and time. The implicit time discretization avoids the severe CFL restriction caused by the large variation in fiber segment lengths. To efficiently solve the resulting, typically ill-conditioned global system, we introduce a two-level overlapping additive Schwarz preconditioner. Under suitable assumptions on the network, we establish uniform convergence of the resulting preconditioned conjugate gradient method. Numerical experiments confirm the theoretical findings.

Abstract: These lecture notes form the first part of a master's-level course on advanced numerical linear algebra. Their aim is not only to present the classical algorithms, but to show why the subject has become considerably more central than it was a generation ago. Numerical linear algebra grew up alongside the numerical solution of partial differential equations, and for a long time that is where its large sparse systems came from. Ranking the nodes of a network, assimilating observations into a weather forecast, and fitting a model to a large noisy data set now lead to problems of the same kind: too large to factorise, structured, and accessible only through matrix-vector products. Strikingly few ideas are needed for all of them. Each chapter therefore develops a standard topic and then puts it to work outside its original setting. We treat norms, factorisations, conditioning and floating-point arithmetic; sparse matrices arising from finite differences, from graphs and from machine learning; stationary iterations and the smoothing property; the conjugate gradient and Lanczos methods, with spectral clustering and regularisation by early stopping; Arnoldi and GMRES, with PageRank and large least squares; and finally preconditioning, Schwarz domain decomposition and multigrid. We assume a first course in linear algebra. Every section closes with a summary of what should be retained and every chapter with exercises, several drawn from past examinations. Accompanying Python code reproduces the numerical illustrations.

</details>

Abstract: We present a comprehensive framework for tangent vector fields and differential operators on the rotation group $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ using harmonic series expansions, which provides a mathematical foundation for their implementation in the crystallographic texture analysis software MTEX. The central idea is to employ the standard left- and right-invariant frames as global orthonormal frames of the tangent bundle, thereby avoiding the numerical instabilities of the classical tangent space basis derived from the Jacobian of the Euler angle parametrization. While the choice of frames is primarily motivated by their geometric and numerical properties, their full potential emerges in the harmonic setting. Representing tangent vector fields through harmonic expansions of their frame components, we derive explicit frequency domain formulas for the gradient, divergence, and curl, allowing these operators to be applied directly to the harmonic coefficients. Moreover, we show that these differential operators preserve harmonic band-limitedness. In addition, the left- and right-invariant representations of tangent vector fields can be transformed into one another directly in the frequency domain, with an increase in harmonic bandwidth of at most one degree.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个统一而简化的有限精度对称Lanczos算法分析框架，覆盖了从失正交现象到向后稳定性的完整理论链条。虽然证明了简单化引入了多项式因子，但该工作显著降低了经典定理的理解门槛，为后续更精细的分析和算法改进奠定了更清晰的理论基础。

Abstract: We give a self-contained finite-precision analysis of the symmetric Lanczos algorithm without reorthogonalization. In particular, we derive the perturbed three-term recurrence, Paige's loss-of-orthogonality identity, containment of all computed Ritz values, and localization of stabilized Ritz values. We then prove a Greenbaum-type backward stability result, exhibiting a nearby problem on which exact Lanczos produces the computed tridiagonal matrix. Our proofs simplify those of Paige and Greenbaum, at the cost of hiding polynomial factors in the iteration count.

</details>

*Francisco R. Villatoro*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究现代Hopfield网络与注意力机制的动力学离散化问题，引入"能量单元"概念，证明在逃逸能级以下，连续流、松弛注意力映射和隐式欧拉的吸引域保持一致，并给出参数统一的收敛界、局部压缩界及最优过松弛准则，还将结果扩展到Bregman几何中的阻尼差凸迭代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代Hopfield网络的检索动力学是log-sum-exp能量的梯度流，而注意力更新是其精确的差凸最小化步骤。已有的离散化方法虽然保持能量衰减和平衡点，但能否保持吸引域这一关键问题尚未被系统研究。理解不同时间离散化对吸引域的影响，对于构建可靠的检索算法至关重要。

Method: 引入"能量单元"（energy cells）概念，即包含一个吸引子且无其他临界点的子水平集的连通分量。通过构造参数一致的单位曲率控制函数导出无条件耗散和离散步骤的单调插值；利用差凸框架分析松弛注意力映射族的动力学性质；推导近良好分离模式下的显式局部压缩界；建立Bregman几何中的阻尼差凸迭代扩展。

Result: 主定理证明：每个低于逃逸能级的有限能量单元同时包含于连续流、所有0&lt;θ&lt;2的松弛注意力映射以及隐式欧拉唯一性区域的吸引域中。此外得到：显式局部压缩界及最优过松弛的认证；近端隧穿和过冲机理描述；一阶误差常数比较；标量重参数化阶数障碍；二阶标量辅助变量格式；以及在Bregman几何中带认证过松弛窗口的扩展。九个数值实验验证了所有界和失效机制。

Conclusion: 在吸引域层面上，连续与离散检索在逃逸能级以下完全一致，所有观察到的离散偏差均发生在数值推断的逃逸能级之上。这为Hopfield网络离散化的可靠性提供了理论保证，也为设计更高阶保持方法指明了方向。

Abstract: The retrieval dynamics of a modern Hopfield network is the gradient flow of a log-sum-exp energy, while the attention update is its exact difference-of-convex minimization step. We study which time discretizations preserve not only energy decay and equilibria but also basins of attraction. We introduce energy cells, connected components of sublevel sets containing one attractor and no other critical point. Our main theorem shows that every finite energy cell below the escape energy is contained simultaneously in the basin of the continuous flow, every relaxed attention map $Ψ_θ=(1-θ)\,\mathrm{id}+θ\,\mathrm{attention}$ for $0<θ<2$, and implicit Euler throughout its uniqueness regime. A parameter-uniform unit-curvature majorant yields unconditional dissipation and a monotone interpolation of each discrete step. We also derive explicit local contraction bounds near well-separated patterns, with a certified optimal slight overrelaxation; characterize proximal tunneling and overshoot beyond the preservation regimes; compare first-order error constants; establish an order barrier for scalar reparametrizations of the relaxed family; construct a second-order scalar-auxiliary-variable scheme; and extend cell preservation to damped difference-of-convex iterations in Bregman geometry, including a certified overrelaxed window under bounded asymmetry. Nine numerical campaigns test the bounds and failure mechanisms. In two-dimensional basin experiments, all observed disagreements between continuous and discrete retrieval occur above the attractor-specific numerically inferred escape level.

</details>

*Vincent K. M. Cheung,Jia-Yeu Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将音乐人形机器人作为研究音乐诱发情绪的实验接口这一新颖视角，主张当前依赖预录音频刺激的研究范式无法捕捉真实音乐体验的多模态、具身与交互特性，而人形机器人通过参数化控制、可复现性和多模态解耦能力，为未来音乐情绪实验提供了方法论平台，并以WAS-5机器人案例验证了技术可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前音乐情绪研究受限于预录音频刺激为主导的实验范式，无法反映真实世界中音乐体验的多模态、具身和交互特性，因此需要一个能够同时操控声学与非声学变量的实验平台来弥补这一缺口。

Method: 本文为立场论文（position paper），基于音乐认知与情绪理论识别需要受控操纵的机制，论证人形机器人在参数化性能变量控制、跨试验可复现性以及听觉/视觉/交互组件的解耦与重组方面的优势，并通过WAS-5吹奏机器人案例研究展示技术可行性。

Result: 通过WAS-5案例研究，证明了人形机器人能够实现对声学与交互变量的可复现控制，满足未来音乐情绪实验的前提条件，验证了该视角在技术上的可行性与可操作性。

Conclusion: 音乐人形机器人应被视为一种方法论平台，而非仅用于表演或人机交互研究，能为音乐诱发情绪研究提供受控、可复现且多模态的实验条件，推动该领域的范式性拓展。

Abstract: Advances in technology have led to increasingly sophisticated musical humanoid robots. However, their use has largely been limited to performance and related research in human-robot interaction. In this position paper, we propose a novel perspective: musical humanoid robots as experimental interfaces for investigating music-evoked emotions. We argue that current research is constrained by paradigms relying on pre-recorded auditory stimuli, which fail to capture the multimodal, embodied, and interactive nature of real-world musical experience. Building on existing theories of music cognition and emotion, we identify mechanisms that require controlled manipulation of both acoustic and non-acoustic variables. We show that humanoid robots are well-suited as they enable parametric control of performance variables, reproducibility across trials, and the decoupling and recombination of auditory, visual, and interactive components. We illustrate the technical feasibility of this perspective through a case study of the WAseda Saxophonist Robot 5 (WAS-5), demonstrating reproducible control of acoustic and interaction variables that are prerequisites for future music-emotion experiments. Our work positions musical humanoid robots as a methodological platform that enables future controlled investigations of music-evoked emotions.

</details>


### [44] [EndoLIFT: Language-Disambiguated Latent-Conditioned Rectified Flow for Bidirectional Endoscopic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20478)
*Chi Kit Ng,Yidong Zhang,Lui Siu Hing,Jinsong Lin,Tianchun Wu,Ho Yin Chim,Zhiqing Tang,Tao Yang,Huxin Gao,Trevor Yeung,Raymond Shing-Yan Tang,Hongliang Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出EndoLIFT，一种用于双向内窥镜控制（推进/撤回）的视觉-语言-动作策略，通过显式语言意图条件化与潜在条件化的整流流动作专家相结合，解决了双向内窥镜控制中的意图别名（intent aliasing）歧义问题。实验表明该方法在导航方向准确率、反向推进抑制、意图跟随鲁棒性以及闭环保真体与离体猪气管测试中均显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规胃肠内窥镜检查本质上是双向的：器械既需要推进到达目标解剖结构，又需要撤回或翻转进行观察，而外部指示可能在视觉场景变化之前就要求反向操作。当请求的相位先于视觉场景发生改变时，几乎相同的视觉观察可能需要完全相反的轴向动作。这种双向内窥镜控制中的歧义性此前未被正式识别和建模，构成了学习的根本障碍。

Method: EndoLIFT将双向内窥镜控制中的歧义形式上化为"意图别名"（intent aliasing），并提出一个视觉-语言-动作策略框架：策略输入RGB图像、语言指令和上一动作状态；采用32维变分轨迹潜变量（VTL）随机条件化连续动作块生成（action-chunk generation），与基于整流流（rectified flow）的动作专家相结合，实现语言驱动意图选择与轨迹潜变量的解耦。

Result: 通过与无潜变量条件的匹配模型对比，EndoLIFT的导航方向准确率提升11.1个百分点，错误方向推进减少83%。在44个未见过语言变体上保持82.8%的意图跟随准确率。在闭环保真体评估中，相对无VTL版本在结肠和未见过的肺/胃仿真体上总体成功率提升30个百分点，并在10/10次离体猪气管试验中全部成功。

Conclusion: 实验验证了语言驱动的轴向模式选择与轨迹潜变量对方向正确性和稳健撤回的贡献是可分离的。EndoLIFT通过显式语言意图条件化解决意图别名歧义，而潜变量条件化进一步增强了方向性正确性和撤回到位的鲁棒性，为双向医疗器械自主控制提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Routine gastrointestinal endoscopy is intrinsically bidirectional: the instrument is advanced to reach target anatomy and later withdrawn or retroflexed for inspection, while an external cue may require earlier reversal. When the requested phase changes before the visual scene does, nearly identical observations can require opposite axial actions. We identify and formalize this ambiguity in bidirectional endoscopic control as intent aliasing. We propose EndoLIFT (Endoscopic Language-Instruction Flow with Trajectory Latents), a vision-language-action policy that combines explicit language-based intent conditioning with a latent-conditioned rectified-flow action expert. The policy receives RGB, a language instruction, and the previous-action state; a 32-D variational trajectory latent stochastically conditions continuous action-chunk generation. Controlled same-observation instruction swaps establish that language selects the axial mode, independently of whether the trajectory latent is present. Relative to the matched model without latent conditioning, EndoLIFT improves navigation-direction accuracy by 11.1 percentage points and reduces wrong-direction advance by 83\%. An architecture-controlled 1-bit mode-flag reference exhibits weaker canonical-anchor switching, while EndoLIFT retains 82.8\% intent-following accuracy across 44 held-out linguistic variants. In closed-loop evaluation, EndoLIFT improves overall success by 30 percentage points over EndoLIFT w/o VTL on both the seen colon phantom and the unseen lung and stomach phantoms, and completes 10/10 ex-vivo porcine-trachea trials. These results separate language-based intent selection from the trajectory latent's contribution to directional correctness and robust retraction.

</details>


### [45] [Koala Gripper: Co-designing Robotic Grippers and Data-Capture Devices for Scaling Dexterous Manipulation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20546)
*Amar Hajj-Ahmad,Zubin Kremer Guha,Tim Fofonoff,Zhi Ern Teoh,Ciarán T. O'Neill,Ben Thacher,Igor Fala,Vidullan Surendran,Murphy Wonsick,Peter Whitney,David Watkins*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个协同设计框架，同时优化数据采集设备和机器人执行装置的设计，并据此开发了Koala Gripper系统——一种兼具人手操控人体工学与机器人抓取性能的夹爪平台。该设计包含力优化的手指/扳机联动机构、单体双拇指结构和人机工学设计，其机器人手指可反向驱动且有效质量仅数十克，支持安全抓取各类物体、有力工具使用和精确分拣，并通过端到端数据采集与策略执行管线验证了从演示学习的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着对更大规模操作数据集需求的增长，手持式机器人夹爪数据采集及其配套夹爪设计变得越来越重要。然而，当前数据采集设备的设计趋势倾向于匹配现有机器人夹爪的形态，牺牲了人体工学和操作性能。因此需要一种协同设计框架，将数据采集平台和机器人执行平台的双重约束同时纳入设计流程。

Method: 提出协同设计（co-design）框架，将数据采集设备与机器人执行装置的需求同时融入设计过程。具体设计了Koala Gripper系统，包括：(1) 新型力优化手指/扳机联动机构，具有定向反射质量特性；(2) 独特的一体式双拇指结构；(3) 以用户为中心的人体工学设计。机器人手指具有反向可驱动性和数十克量级的有效质量。

Result: Koala Gripper系统相比平行夹爪提升了灵巧性和抓取能力，同时保持了可扩展性和易用性。实验表明该夹爪能够在广泛物体范围内实现安全抓取、有力工具使用和精确分拣。通过端到端数据采集和策略执行管线，验证了其在从演示学习中发挥的能力。

Conclusion: 通过协同设计框架将数据采集工具和机器人执行装置统一设计是可行的，Koala Gripper验证了这一思路的有效性，既保留了手持采集的人体工学和操作性能，又确保了机器人侧的执行能力和安全性，为大规模操作数据集的构建提供了新路径。

Abstract: As the demand for larger manipulation datasets grows, handheld robotic gripper data collection and the associated gripper designs become more vital. Current data collection device designs trend towards matching the morphologies of existing robotic grippers, sacrificing ergonomics and manipulation performance. In this paper, we propose a co-design framework that guides the simultaneous development of both data collection and robotic execution devices by weaving both platform constraints into the design process. Through this workflow, we present the Koala Gripper system, a data capture device and robotic gripper platform that improves dexterity and grasp capability compared to parallel jaw grippers while preserving scalability and ease-of-use. The design introduces a novel force-optimized finger/trigger linkage mechanism with directional reflected mass characteristics, a unique monolithic dual-thumb, and user-centered ergonomic design. The design's actuated robotic fingers are backdrivable, with effective mass on the order of tens of grams. We show that these grippers are capable of secure grasps over a wide range of objects, forceful tool use, and precise singulation. We further validate the platform by deploying it with an end-to-end data collection and policy execution pipeline that highlights its capabilities through learning from demonstration. More information available at http://koalagripper.rai-inst.com

</details>


### [46] [Logic-VLA: A Temporal Logic Conditioned Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20556)
*Celina Shiyu Wang,Yiqi Zhao,Junjie Ye,Yue Wang,Jyotirmoy V. Deshmukh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Logic-VLA是一个形式需求感知的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，通过在推理时输入信号时序逻辑（STL）规范来调度行为。它采用基于语法图的STL编码器，并通过两阶段训练（STL条件监督微调+轨迹级偏好优化）实现，在四旋翼导航仿真中将STL满足率提升24.8-40.7个百分点，同时将自然语言任务成功率损失控制在1.8个百分点以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型虽能遵循自然语言指令，但自然语言表达可能无法精确指定安全关键或时空约束要求（如时序、位置等），导致行为在正式需求层面不可保证。需要一个能够显式利用形式化规范（如STL）来引导策略生成的方法。

Method: Logic-VLA包含三个核心组件：(1) 基于语法图的STL编码器，通过预训练捕获时序逻辑语义；(2) 两阶段策略适应：第一阶段为STL条件的有监督微调（利用满足规范的演示数据），第二阶段采用基于流匹配代理的Identity Preference Optimization，在满足/违反配对的轨迹级别上进行偏好优化；(3) 推理时直接输入STL公式作为条件，使策略根据具体形式化需求动态调整行为。

Result: 在闭环四旋翼导航仿真（随机化逼真环境）中，相比STL不可感知的基线策略，Logic-VLA将STL满足率提高了24.8-40.7个百分点，而自然语言任务成功率最多仅下降1.8个百分点，并能泛化到训练中未见过的STL公式。

Conclusion: 实验表明，单个VLA模型即可根据不同的形式化需求自适应调整行为，无需为每种规范单独训练策略，实现了形式需求满足与自然语言任务执行之间的良好平衡。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models can follow natural-language (NL) task instructions, but such instructions may not precisely specify safety-critical or spatiotemporal requirements on the resulting behavior. We introduce Logic-VLA, a formal-requirement-aware VLA that conditions on Signal Temporal Logic (STL) specifications supplied at inference time. Logic-VLA uses a syntax-graph-based STL encoder pre-trained to capture temporal logic semantics. Policy adaptation proceeds in two stages: STL-conditioned supervised fine-tuning on satisfying demonstrations is followed by trajectory-level preference optimization over matched satisfying-violating rollout pairs using a flow-matching surrogate for Identity Preference Optimization. This formulation improves formal requirement satisfaction while preserving the nominal NL task. We evaluate Logic-VLA in closed-loop quadcopter navigation simulation across randomized photorealistic environments and test generalization to STL formulas unseen during training. Across the evaluation benchmarks, Logic-VLA improves STL satisfaction rate over an STL-blind base policy by 24.8 to 40.7 percentage points (pp) while reducing nominal NL task success by at most 1.8 pp, showing that a single VLA can adapt its behavior to varying formal requirements without requiring a separate policy for each specification.

</details>

*Nishanth Bobbili,Pratyaksh Rao,Luca Morando,Luca Masci,Giuseppe Loianno*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Planning and control of fixed-wing Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) are challenging due to nonlinear dynamics, aerodynamic limits, and environmental disturbances. Differential flatness offers a principled way to generate fast, feasible trajectories, but its use has largely been confined to model-free controllers, which lack predictive capabilities and demand tuning. In this paper, we propose a unified framework that integrates differential flatness-based trajectory generation with Nonlinear Model Predictive Control (NMPC), combining computationally efficient planning with predictive, constraint-aware control. To further improve robustness, we introduce a wind-aware sampling strategy embedded within the NMPC framework, enabling the generation of dynamically feasible reference trajectories that proactively account for wind disturbances while strictly enforcing aerodynamic and control input constraints. We validate the proposed framework through extensive simulations and real-world flight experiments, demonstrating improved tracking accuracy and robustness for complex trajectories, particularly when using the proposed wind-aware sampling strategy under strong wind conditions.

</details>


### [48] [Rethinking Demonstration Unlearning in Imitation Learning for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20784)
*Jiazhuo Li,Yu Zhang,Yiming Fei,Kangkang Dong,Xiaojun Zhu,Houde Liu,Jinze Tao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向机器人模仿学习的"重训练校准审核"框架，用于评估从已训练策略中移除人类演示数据（即演示反学习）的效果。框架沿两个轴进行审核：行为轴（编辑后的策略是否行为上接近未含被删演示的重训练策略）和证据轴（审计者是否仍能检测出该策略曾用被删数据训练）。通过独立重训练舰队构建校准基线，并用一致性检验综合两轴假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人模仿学习依赖人类演示数据，用户可能事后要求删除部分数据。重新训练是自然参考但成本随策略和数据集规模增长，需要更低成本的编辑算子。然而现有机器学习反学习指标（如遗忘损失或单一成员攻击）无法有效评估闭环策略中被移除的具体内容，因此需要一个重训练校准的审核方法来严格评估反学习效果。

Method: 提出双轴审核框架：(1) 行为轴：在被匹配的状态下，测量编辑策略与重训练策略的动作散度，以独立重训练之间的散度作为地板（floor）校准；(2) 证据轴：对每个演示应用针对重训练零分布的成员攻击，同时报告排名和绝对成员损失水平。最后用符合性检验（conformal test）将两轴结合为联合重训练一致性的单一假设，基于足够大的独立重训练舰队在常规显著性水平下进行检验。

Result: 在三种真实机器人策略类别和两个模拟套件的五个预注册条件下，两轴在一个检查点上双向解耦：一个编辑可能修复任务行为但证据不变，或降低证据但行为偏离重训练。在ACT分支上，重定向编辑将盲评分机器人在20次试验中的成功次数恢复到18次。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了仅依赖现有机器学习反学习指标不足以评估机器人闭环策略的演示数据移除效果，所提出的重训练校准审核框架提供了一种更严格、可验证的手段，能够验证编辑算子是否真正达到了与重训练一致的效果，并揭示了行为与证据两个维度的独立性。

Abstract: Imitation learning for robotics depends on human demonstrations, some of which people may later ask to remove. Retraining without them is the natural reference, but its cost grows with policy and dataset scale, motivating cheaper operators that edit a trained policy. Metrics inherited from machine unlearning, such as forgetting loss or a single membership attack, do not establish what an edit removed from a policy acting in closed loop. We therefore introduce a retrain-calibrated audit that reads demonstration unlearning along two axes: behavior, whether the edited policy acts like one retrained without the removed demonstrations, and evidence, whether an auditor can still detect it was trained on them. The behavior axis measures action divergence to that retrain at matched states, calibrated by a floor built from independent retrains, so a policy at the floor is as close to a retrain as retrains are to each other. The evidence axis applies a per-demonstration membership attack against a retrain null, reporting both its rank and its absolute member-loss level, since rank alone accepts operators that inflate member losses past the null. A conformal test then combines both axes into one hypothesis of joint retrain consistency, against a fleet of independent retrains large enough to reject at conventional significance. Across five preregistered conditions on three real-robot policy classes and two simulation suites, the axes dissociate in both directions on one checkpoint, as an edit may repair task behavior while leaving evidence unchanged, or reduce evidence while moving behavior away from retraining. On the ACT arm, a redirect edit restores blind-scored robot success to 18 of 20 trials.

</details>


### [49] [Natural Sit-to-Stand Motion Synthesis For Humanoids via Guided Assistance Curricula and Staged Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20823)
*Meet Pal Singh,Vyankatesh Ashtekar,Ashish Dutta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于强化学习的仿人机器人从坐姿到站姿（STS）运动合成方法，无需任何演示或参考轨迹，仅通过单一PPO策略即可生成平滑、类人的起立动作，在八种椅子高度上达到97%以上的平衡站立成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿人机器人起立（STS）是一个高维、多约束的控制难题，因为存在无穷多种保持平衡的起立方式。现有方法通常依赖运动捕捉数据或参考轨迹，限制了泛化能力和实用价值，因此需要一种无需演示即可从零合成自然起立动作的方法。

Method: 采用强化学习从零合成STS运动，核心由三个互补组件构成：(i) 耦合力/椅高课程：垂直骨盆辅助力在训练初期辅助轨迹探索并随训练衰减，同时逐步解锁更高的椅子，避免过早的分布偏移导致泛化崩塌；(ii) 运动鲁棒性：从逆运动学生成的大规模初始和目标姿态中随机采样，覆盖八种以上椅高；(iii) 生物力学与最优控制启发的奖励设计：塑形坐离时刻的角动量，并通过质心压力吸引函数实现支撑区域转换，确保平滑低功耗的关节驱动。

Result: 在确定性无外力评估器上，策略在八种椅子高度上的平衡站立成功率超过97%。策略能够在不同椅高之间泛化出平滑动作，相比于现有技术，机器人能够从更深的坐姿姿态成功起立。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了通过精心设计的课程学习、姿态多样化采样和生物力学启发奖励，强化学习可以在无任何外部参考数据的情况下合成自然且泛化能力强的仿人STS运动，超越了现有方法在深坐姿场景下的表现。

Abstract: A humanoid has infinitely many ways to stand up from sitting while maintaining balance, making sit-to-stand (STS) a challenging control problem. We synthesise natural humanoid STS motion from scratch using reinforcement learning, without demonstrations or reference trajectories. A single Proximal Policy Optimisation policy learns smooth, human-like rising driven by three complementary components. (i) A coupled force/chair-height curriculum is used. A vertical pelvis-assist force aids early trajectory exploration and decays over training. Taller chairs are unlocked with decaying assisting force. This ensures that the policy masters a viable STS trajectory at each chair height before being exposed to harder ones, avoiding the premature distribution shift that otherwise collapses generalisation. (ii) Motion robustness is achieved by randomly sampling from a large number of inverse kinematics-generated initial and target poses spanning over eight chair heights. (iii) A set of rewards is defined inspired from biomechanics and optimal control studies. They shape the robot's angular momentum for seat-off, and enable support-region transition via centre of pressure attraction function to ensure smooth low-effort actuation. On a deterministic force-free evaluator, the policy attains more than 97% balanced-standing success across eight chair heights. The policy generalises smooth motion across chair heights and enables the robot to rise from substantially deep-seated postures as compared to the state of the art.

</details>


### [50] [Demonstration-Guided Humanoid Stand-Up on an Emulated Deformable Surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20852)
*Aniruddh Kushwah,Vyankatesh Ashtekar,Ashish Dutta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种参考引导的强化学习框架，利用在硬地面录制的人类演示来生成29自由度Unitree G1人形机器人在可变形的软地面上的站立动作。通过MuJoCo的solref和solimp参数建模地形柔顺性，先基于参考追踪和显式恢复目标训练策略，再降低地形刚度使策略适应软土地面，最终成功完成从跌倒到站立的任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在软质地面（如草地、沙地或垫层）上进行站立动作比硬地面更具挑战性，因为地面下陷会导致支撑力延迟生成、接触动力学复杂化。现有的强化学习方法通常假设刚性地面，缺乏对地形柔顺性的建模与适应，需要一种参考引导的框架使机器人能够在柔软地面完成跌倒恢复任务。

Method: 使用MuJoCo的solref和solimp参数建模软接触地形；奖励由两部分组成：通过残差关节位置控制追踪参考动作，以及显式恢复目标（骨盆高度、躯干直立度、最终姿态）。训练分两阶段：先在硬地面上训练策略，然后通过更新solref降低地形刚度、扩大solimp名义穿透区域，进行后续训练使策略适应穿透导致的延迟支撑力，同时保持原始演示模式。

Result: 学习到的策略在仿真中成功完成从跌倒到站立的任务，达到目标骨盆高度和直立度，最大接触穿透约40毫米。在两个站立序列上均成功，在硬地面和软地面都能实现最终恢复目标。消融实验表明仅参考追踪不足以完成站立，显式恢复奖励至关重要。

Conclusion: 参考引导的强化学习框架结合显式恢复目标能够有效使29自由度人形机器人在软质可变形地面上完成站立任务。该方法通过两阶段训练（先硬后软）实现了策略对地形柔顺性的自适应，验证了显式恢复奖励在跌倒恢复任务中的必要性。

Abstract: This paper presents a reference-guided reinforcement learning framework to generate stand-up motion for a 29-DOF Unitree G1 humanoid on deformable soft ground, using a human demonstration recorded on hard ground. The terrain compliance is modelled using solref and solimp parameters from MuJoCo's rigid body soft-contact model. The rewards consists of (i) reference motion tracking through residual joint-position control and (ii) explicit recovery objectives such as pelvis height, torso uprightness, and the final posture. First, the policy is trained with the specified rewards considering hard ground. Next, the terrain stiffness is lowered by updating solref and the nominal surface penetration zone is expanded using solimp. Subsequent training enables the policy to adapt to the delayed support force generation due to significant surface penetration during contact-intensive phases while preserving the original demonstration pattern. The learned policy successfully completes the fallen-to-standing task in simulation, reaching the targeted pelvis height and uprightness, with a maximum contact penetration of approximately 40 mm during the process. The proposed method is demonstrated on two stand-up sequences and successfully achieves the final recovery objective on both hard and soft ground. Ablation studies show that reference tracking alone is insufficient for successful stand-up, and that explicit recovery rewards are essential.

</details>


### [51] [IMU-Free Body-Frame State Estimation with Sparse Scene Flow for Quadcopters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20891)
*Daniel Grønhaug,Sofie Markeset,Mathias Kolberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种纯视觉的X型四旋翼状态估计系统，仅依赖同步立体图像与电机推力指令，在无IMU和GPS的情况下，于机体坐标系中完成位姿、速度、角速度、重力及扰动的估计，并输出稀疏场景流与自适应稀疏点云。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统无人机状态估计高度依赖IMU和GPS，但在无GPS环境（如室内、隧道）或低成本平台中，IMU不可用或精度不足时缺乏可靠的机体状态信息来源；同时现有视觉惯性系统需在IMU与视觉之间进行复杂标定与同步，增加系统复杂度。因此需要一种仅依赖视觉输入即可进行机体状态估计的方案，以适应无惯性传感器配置的X构型四旋翼平台。

Method: 该方法构建了一个连续-离散扩展卡尔曼滤波器，状态定义在复合流形<SE(3), R^3, ...>上，在机体坐标系中联合估计位姿、速度、角速度、重力和扰动。利用静止场景点作为隐式惯性参考，通过FAST/Shi-Tomasi检测、SSD/Lucas-Kanade跟踪、NCC双目匹配提取特征，利用滤波器预测搜索区域，并通过卡方门控的归一化创新量仅接纳静止点。同时，系统通过4视图（两个时间戳的双目对）全束集调整联合估计点位置与速度，利用立体视差和时序视差，以滤波器位姿为先验，生成携带逐点位置、速度及联合协方差的稀疏点云，密度由外部焦点自适应分配。

Result: 系统实现了无IMU条件下的机体坐标系视觉状态估计，输出机体状态估计、标定后的位姿变化及稀疏场景流；生成空间自适应的稀疏3D点云（含逐点位置、速度与联合协方差），在关注区域提供密集覆盖而在其他区域保持稀疏；该架构为下游以当前机体坐标系锚定的世界模型提供测量源。

Conclusion: 该系统证明了一种无需惯性传感器和世界坐标系基础设施即可进行四旋翼状态估计的可行性，通过立体视觉配合推力指令在机体框架内自包含地完成状态估计与场景流构建；架构开放性好，为未来引入GPS、IMU或世界帧信息预留了接口。

Abstract: We present a vision-only state estimation system for X-configuration quadcopters equipped with a canonical stereo camera pair and no inertial sensors. The system operates entirely in the body frame, requiring only synchronised stereo images and motor thrust commands. A continuous-discrete extended Kalman filter on a composite manifold state $\langle SE(3), \mathbb{R}^3, \ldots \rangle$ maintains estimates of body-frame pose, velocity, angular velocity, gravity, and disturbances, using stationary scene points as implicit inertial references. Feature points are detected (FAST, Shi-Tomasi), tracked temporally (SSD, Lucas-Kanade) and matched across cameras (NCC), with search regions predicted from filter-derived pose and point uncertainty. Chi-squared gating on the normalised innovation admits only stationary points to the filter. The system also produces a sparse 3D point cloud carrying per-point position, velocity and joint covariance. These come from a 4-view (two stereo pairs at two timestamps) full bundle adjustment that jointly estimates position and velocity from stereo disparity and temporal parallax, with the filter-derived relative pose as a prior. Feature points in the EKF do not enter the solver; their information is reflected through the pose prior. Point cloud density is spatially adaptive: an external focus point directs allocation, producing dense coverage in the region of attention and sparse coverage elsewhere. The output is a body-frame state estimate, a calibrated pose change, and a sparse scene flow. It is intended as a measurement source for a downstream world model anchored in the current body frame, without dependence on GPS, IMU, or any world-frame infrastructure, though the architecture accommodates their future integration.

</details>


### [52] [Scalable Distributed Simulation-Based Testing for Automated Driving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20904)
*Christian Geller,Benedikt Haas,Lutz Eckstein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个端到端、DevOps驱动的框架，用于在轻量级Kubernetes集群上自动化CARLA场景测试的构建、部署和分布式执行。通过Helm charts、Helmfile清单及Argo Workflows实现ROS 2应用的标准化打包与并行场景测试，在200个场景的评估中，最优配置相比顺序基线将端到端工作流时间加速超过8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶系统（ADS）和智能交通系统（ITS）的虚拟场景测试是关键验证手段，但大规模测试套件（可能包含数千个场景）的执行为人工密集且难以扩展。现有方法缺乏端到端的自动化与分布式执行能力，亟需一个可扩展且可追溯的测试框架。

Method: （1）将ROS 2应用包装为从仓库规范自动生成的标准Kubernetes Helm charts；（2）通过动态Helmfile清单声明式组合完整仿真环境；（3）利用Argo Workflows实现分布式测试工作流，包括环境部署、OpenSCENARIO测试用例的聚合与批处理、跨集群节点的并行场景执行、日志与资源指标收集；（4）在K3s多节点集群上评估200个场景的性能。

Result: 在多节点K3s集群上运行200个场景的评估中，最佳配置相比顺序基线将端到端工作流时间加速超过8倍；结果量化了并行度、编排开销与集群稳定性之间的权衡；并在真实ADS测试应用中与场景源和下游评估模块集成验证。

Conclusion: 该框架不仅为可扩展的仿真测试提供了坚实基础，还能生成可追溯的证据以支持安全论证，为ADS和ITS的大规模虚拟验证提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Virtual scenario-based testing is a key enabler for validating automated driving systems (ADS) and intelligent transport systems (ITS). However, executing large-scale test suites involving possibly thousands of scenarios remains labor-intensive and difficult to scale. This paper presents an end-to-end, DevOps-driven framework that automates build, deployment, and distributed execution of CARLA-based scenario tests of an ADS on a lightweight Kubernetes cluster. ROS 2 applications are packaged as standardized Kubernetes Helm charts generated from repository specifications, while entire simulation environments are composed declaratively via dynamic Helmfile manifests. The paper describes how a distributed testing workflow can be implemented in Argo Workflows to provision environments, aggregate and batch OpenSCENARIO test cases from configurable sources, execute scenarios in parallel across cluster nodes, and collect logs and resource metrics. In an evaluation on a multi-node K3s cluster running 200 scenarios, the best configuration speeds up end-to-end workflow time by more than a factor of eight compared to a sequential baseline. The results demonstrate significant gains in end-to-end execution time and quantify trade-offs between parallelism, orchestration overhead, and cluster stability. The framework is further demonstrated in a real-world ADS test application with connections to scenario sources and downstream evaluation modules. This demonstrates that the approach provides a strong foundation not only for scalable simulation testing, but also for generating traceable evidence that can support safety arguments.

</details>
