### [94] [EWOCS VII : Unveiling the faint diffuse X-ray emission in Westerlund 1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21076)
*Albacete-Colombo J. F.,Andersen M.,De Becker M.,Mackey J.,Larkin C. J. K.,Guarcello M. G.,Anastasopoulou K.,Sciortino S.,Greco E.,Miceli M.,Sapienza V.,Flaccomio E.,Filócomo A.,Stevens I. A.,Bayo A.,Drake J. J.,Gennaro M.,Gunderson S. J.,Fraschetti F*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用36次Chandra ACIS-I观测对银河系最近的大质量星团Westerlund 1进行了最详细的弥散X射线研究。通过剔除4922个点源、扣除PSF翼污染并校正背景后，发现弥散X射线主要由激波加热的热等离子体和电荷交换发射（CXE）驱动，核心区温度约1.8-4.1 keV，外区温度较软，总弥散光度约9.6×10^33 erg/s，未发现非热成分。团风模型预测光度高于观测约2.2倍，表明星团内介质处于部分掏空的低密度状态，风转X射线效率比Cygnus OB2低约两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Westerlund 1作为距离太阳最近、包含超过10万颗各类光谱型恒星（下至褐矮星）的超大质量星团，其恒星群强烈加热周围星际介质，是研究恒星反馈对团内气体影响的关键场所。此前缺乏对其弥散X射线发射的点源贡献分离与量化，需要对真实弥散发射进行细致观测与分析。

Method: 分析36次Chandra ACIS-I观测，窗口为8×8角分。使用ACIS Extract基于能量依赖的PSF模型和自适应99%包裹能量掩模移除4922个点源，扣除PSF翼污染并校正背景，随后在软、中、硬能带进行自适应平滑。光谱拟合采用APEC+PSHOCK与CXE+NEI模型，并与团风模型对照。

Result: 核心区（Region #2）由APEC+PSHOCK模型拟合，温度分别为1.81±0.24和4.11±1.98 keV，并探测到6.7 keV Fe Kα线；外区（Region #1）显示电荷交换发射，CXE+NEI模型给出较软温度0.27±0.04和1.09±0.05 keV。柱密度范围1.84-2.24×10^22 cm^-2，总（0.5-8.0 keV）弥散光度约9.6±0.3×10^33 erg/s。未发现非热贡献。团风模型预测光度为观测值的约2.2倍，风转X射线效率η约4.3×10^-6，比Cygnus OB2低约两个数量级。

Conclusion: Wd 1的弥散X射线发射主要由两个物理过程驱动：核心区来自热化的Wolf-Rayet风及风-风碰撞产生的硬X射线，因绝热膨胀和与较冷ISM的湍流混合而软化；外区则来自低消光区域的电荷交换发射所贡献的软成分。观测光度低于团风模型预测，表明团内介质为部分掏空的低密度状态，导致该星团的弥散X射线发射相对低亮（subluminous），风转X射线效率远低于Cygnus OB2。

Abstract: Westerlund 1 (Wd 1) is the closest supermassive star cluster to the Sun, with over 100,000 stars of all spectral types down to brown dwarfs. This population strongly heats the surrounding interstellar medium (ISM), making Wd 1 a key site to study stellar feedback on intracluster gas. We present the most detailed X-ray study to date of its diffuse emission, aiming to separate and quantify the point-source contribution to the true diffuse emission. We analysed 36 Chandra ACIS-I observations within an 8x8 arcmin window. After removing 4922 point sources with ACIS Extract using energy-dependent PSF models and adaptive 99% enclosed-energy masks, we subtracted PSF-wing contamination and corrected for background, then applied adaptive smoothing in soft, medium, and hard bands. Spectral fitting suggests a shocked thermal plasma, with diffuse X-rays correlating spatially with massive stars. The core (Region #2) fits an APEC+PSHOCK model, with temperatures of 1.81+/-0.24 and 4.11+/-1.98 keV and a 6.7 keV Fe K-alpha line. The outer Region #1 shows charge-exchange emission, fitting a CXE+NEI model with softer temperatures of 0.27+/-0.04 and 1.09+/-0.05 keV. Column densities range from 1.84-2.24x10^22 cm^-2, and the total (0.5-8.0 keV) diffuse luminosity is ~9.6(+/-0.3)x10^33 erg/s. Near the core, hard emission likely arises from thermalised Wolf-Rayet winds and wind-wind collisions, softened by adiabatic expansion and turbulent mixing with cooler ISM. The soft component extends farther out, possibly from CXE in lower-extinction regions. No non-thermal contribution is found. A cluster-wind model predicts a luminosity ~2.2 above that observed, consistent with a low-density, partially evacuated intracluster medium. The resulting wind-to-X-ray efficiency, eta ~4.3x10^-6, is about two orders of magnitude below Cygnus OB2, yielding comparatively subluminous diffuse X-ray emission.

</details>


### [95] [CtaAgnVar : a Pipeline for Variability Studies with the Cherenkov Telescope Array Observatory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21158)
*G. Grolleron,J. -P. Lenain,J. Biteau,C. Boisson,M. Cerruti*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了CtaAgnVar，一个基于Gammapy的开源管道，用于模拟和重建CTAO对耀变体（blazars）等活动星系核（AGN）在甚高能波段的时间变化观测。通过对PG 1553+113超过15年的模拟观测，验证了CTAO能以超过5σ显著性恢复其周期信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耀变体是活动星系核中相对论性喷流向观测者方向对准的特殊天体，其能谱和通量变化是研究中心黑洞及喷流物理的关键。CTAO作为下一代成像大气切伦科夫望远镜阵列，其灵敏度比现有设备提高5-10倍，有望以空前精度重建AGN在甚高能波段的时变特性。需要开发一套完整的模拟-重建管道来量化CTAO对耀变体和瞬变源的观测能力。

Method: 开发了CtaAgnVar管道，基于Gammapy（CTAO科学分析工具的核心Python库），覆盖从时变谱能量分布（SED）到模拟真实观测序列（考虑源可见性）再到重建能谱和光变曲线的完整链条。提供多种变率估计量，包括一个新的统计估计量用于检测硬度比图中的磁滞回线模式。管道以开源形式发布，仪器仅通过响应函数和阵列位置参数进入模拟，不限于CTAO使用。

Result: 对PG 1553+113模拟了超过15年的CTAO观测。假设甚高能辐射跟随高能辐射，CTAO以每周30分钟的观测节奏在15年内能以超过5σ显著性恢复该源的周期信号，周期确定精度主要受限于光变曲线采样而非光子统计量。

Conclusion: CtaAgnVar作为一个开源的、仪器无关的模拟-重建管道，能够有效量化CTAO对耀变体时变观测的能力。结果验证了CTAO在甚高能波段对AGN周期信号探测的优越性能，其周期测量的极限将由观测调度策略决定。

Abstract: Blazars are active galactic nuclei whose relativistic jet points toward the observer, accelerating particles that radiate across the electromagnetic spectrum. Characterizing their flux and spectral variability constrains the physics of the central black hole and its jet, making blazars a key science case for the Cherenkov Telescope Array Observatory (CTAO). The CTAO, with two sites and a sensitivity five to ten times better than current Imaging Atmospheric Cherenkov Telescopes, should reconstruct AGN variability at very high energies with unprecedented precision. To quantify these capabilities for blazars and transients, we developed CtaAgnVar, a pipeline based on Gammapy, the open-source Python package for gamma-ray analysis selected as core library of the CTAO Science Analysis Tools. It covers the full chain from a time-dependent spectral energy distribution to the simulation of a realistic observation sequence, accounting for source visibility, and to the reconstruction of spectra and light curves. It provides variability estimators, including a new statistical estimator quantifying the detection of hysteresis patterns in hardness-ratio diagrams. Released as open source, it lets the instrument enter only through the response functions and array location, so its use is not restricted to the CTAO. We simulated over 15 years of CTAO observations of PG 1553+113, whose high-energy emission shows a periodicity in Fermi-LAT data. Assuming the very-high-energy emission follows the high-energy one, the CTAO would recover this periodicity above 5 sigma with 15 years of 30 min observations on a weekly cadence, the period determination being then limited by the light-curve sampling rather than by photon statistics.

</details>


### [96] [Constraining neutron-star properties with ensembles of thermonuclear bursts: application to SRGA J144459.2-604207](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21196)
*Duncan K. Galloway,Serena Jones,Luke Waterson,Tao Fu,Adelle J. Goodwin,Zhaosheng Li*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了BEANSP包的进一步发展，新增"ensemble"分析模式用于利用"clocked"热核X射线暴对中子星性质进行约束。将该方法应用于GS 1826-24及吸积动力毫秒脉冲星SRGA J144459.2-604207（2024年爆发期间14天epoch数据），推断出其燃料氢丰度约0.54、CNO金属丰度约0.01，距离约6-11 kpc。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从热核(I型)X射线暴推断宿主中子星性质面临挑战，主要源于数据不完整、参数空间多维以及缺乏合适的模型与分析工具。

Method: 开发BEANSP包的"ensemble"分析模式，利用部分源展现的规律性"clocked"爆发行为，每个epoch仅需一次模型评估（较原"train"模式更高效）；采用KEPLER模型网格，并在模拟数据上测试参数恢复能力，随后应用到GS 1826-24和SRGA J144459.2-604207的真实观测数据。

Result: 对GS 1826-24的结果与先前研究一致；模拟实验可较好地恢复与爆发点火相关的输入参数，但对系统距离、发射各向异性和中子星质量半径的恢复精度较低；对SRGA J144459.2-604207推断出亚太阳燃料成分（X≈0.54, Z_CNO≈0.01），但与单独确定的0.4氢丰度上限存在冲突，距离范围估计为6-11 kpc。

Conclusion: "ensemble"模式为利用clocked爆发约束中子星性质提供了高效工具，但当前模型在某些爆发点火区间的预测精度有限；未来采用更物理真实的模型对富氢爆发源（该源及其他类似源）具有广阔应用前景。

Abstract: Deducing the properties of the host neutron stars from thermonuclear (type-I) X-ray bursts remains a challenge, due to incomplete data, multidimensional parameter space, and dearth of suitable models and analysis tools. Here we describe further development of the BEANSP package, with a new "ensemble" analysis mode utilising the consistent and regular "clocked" bursting exhibited by some sources. This mode requires only one model evaluation per epoch, and so is much more efficient than the previous "train" mode. We apply the code to the best-known source exhibiting "clocked" bursting, GS 1826-24, utilising a grid of KEPLER models pre-calculated for this purpose, and find good agreement with a previous study. We performed experiments on simulated data, and recovered input parameters related to the burst ignition with reasonable accuracy, but less so for the system distance, emission anisotropy and neutron star mass and radius. Finally, we assembled a set of 14 daily burst epochs covering the 2024 outburst of the accretion-powered millisecond pulsar SRGA J144459.2-604207, and attempted to constrain the system properties of this object by comparing to SETTLE model predictions. We find reasonably good agreement between the observations and model predictions for a mildly sub-solar fuel composition, with H-fraction $X\approx0.54$ and CNO metallicity $Z_{\rm CNO}\approx0.01$. However, we note that the inferred H-fraction is in excess of the limit of 0.4 established separately, and the adopted model may not provide sufficiently accurate predictions for this burst ignition regime. The inferred distance depends on assumptions about the system inclination and corresponding anisotropy of the persistent emission, and is likely in the range 6-11 kpc. Future applications with more physically realistic models are a promising avenue for this and other sources with H-rich bursts.

</details>


### [97] [Generation of TeV Photons by PeV Neutrinos in Dense Astrophysical Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21266)
*Jun-Chen Wang,Hanlin Song,Hao Li,Jie Zhu,Bo-Qiang Ma*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文计算了超高能中微子与核子相互作用产生超高能光子的机制，发现能量超过1 PeV的中微子有超过13%的概率产生能量超过1 TeV的光子，并将其应用于GRB 221009A爆发前TeV光子的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IceCube和KM3Net探测到PeV尺度超高能中微子，以及多个地面观测站（Tibet ASγ、MAGIC、Carpet-3、LHAASO等）探测到TeV-PeV光子，这些发现表明极端天体物理环境中存在粒子加速到超高能的能力，促使研究者探索超高能中微子与光子之间的联系。

Method: 计算中微子-核子相互作用产生超高能光子的过程：入射中微子与核子散射，产生次级部分子，部分子强子化为π介子，π介子衰变产生超高能光子，并计算相应的光子能谱。

Result: 对于能量超过1 PeV的入射(反)中微子，产生能量超过1 TeV光子的概率大于13%；该机制可定量解释GRB 221009A中爆前TeV光子的能量和提前时间。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了将超高能中微子事件与伽马射线观测联系起来的可行机制，为极端天体物理环境中的强子过程提供了新见解，支持多信使天文学研究。

Abstract: Recent observations by IceCube and KM3Net of PeV-scale ultra-high-energy (UHE) neutrinos, together with detections of TeV-PeV photons from various sources such as the Crab Nebula, the Galactic Center, and gamma-ray burst by ground-based observatories including Tibet AS$γ$, MAGIC, Carpet-3, and LHAASO, point to the existence of extreme astrophysical environments capable of accelerating particles to ultra-high energies. These findings motivate investigations of possible connections between UHE neutrinos and photons in such environments. Theoretically, dense regions surrounding compact objects can efficiently produce UHE neutrinos. In this work, we calculate the production of UHE photons from neutrino-nucleon interactions, and note that if these interactions occur in the outer, optically thin regions of dense environments, the resulting photons could potentially be observed. In our model, an incident neutrino scatters off a nucleon, generating secondary partons that hadronize into pions and subsequently decay into UHE photons. We calculate the resulting photon energy spectra and find that for incident (anti)neutrinos with energies above 1 PeV, the probability of producing photons with energies exceeding 1 TeV is greater than 13%. As a concrete application, we show that this mechanism can quantitatively account for the preburst TeV photons observed in GRB 221009A, providing a natural explanation for both their energies and lead times. These findings establish a plausible mechanism linking UHE neutrino events to gamma-ray observations, providing new insights into hadronic processes in extreme astrophysical environments and supporting multi-messenger astronomy studies.

</details>


### [98] [VLASS Discovery of a Luminous Galactic Radio Transient Evolving on Decade Timescales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21320)
*Jessie M. Miller,Gregg Hallinan,Dillon Dong,Adolfo S. Carvalho,S. T. Myers,Jean Somalwar,Casey Law,B. M. Gaensler,Vikram Ravi,Laura Chomiuk,Assaf Horesh,Delina Levine,Yuyang Chen*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文报道了一个多波段分析结果：瞬变射电源VT J1906+0849，该源在VLASS Epoch 1中以70 mJy被探测到，距NVSS未探测事件21年后。射电观测显示该源于2005年首次探测，2014年达到≥200 mJy峰值，随后衰减至2025年底再增亮。该源位于银河系纬度约0.74°，光学-红外对应体性质支持银河系起源。作者提出其极端的射电光度可能由致密天体上的持续吸积驱动，但Swift-XRT未探测显示其相对于银河系X射线双星群体而言X射线昏暗。作者认为该源可能是微类星体SS 433的年轻类似体，具有稠密盘风持续供能的同步辐射外流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别并分类一颗新型的、性质无法匹配任何已知银河系射电源类别的瞬变射电天体VT J1906+0849，该源在时域、谱域和X射线表现上均与已知X射线双星和微类星体不同，需要对其物理机制和起源进行深入理解。

Method: 采用多波段分析方法，综合VLASS和NVSS射电巡天数据追踪源的长期光变，结合射电宽带光谱分析（同步自吸收模型）、甚长基线干涉测量（VLBI）约束源区膨胀、近红外光谱鉴别Brγ发射线特征，并结合Swift-XRT观测进行X射线光度约束。

Result: VT J1906+0849表现为一个射电瞬变源，2014年峰值≥200 mJy，源区在5年以上几乎没有膨胀；近红外谱呈现单一宽发射线（蓝移Brγ），对应约2000 km/s的持续非对称外流；X射线方面相对于银河系X射线双星明显昏暗。

Conclusion: VT J1906+0849可能是一种前所未见的银河系射电源类型，最可能的解释是微类星体SS 433的年轻类似体：稠密盘风限制并持续供能一个紧凑的同步辐射外流，射电-恒星风相互作用解释了其紧凑、缓慢膨胀的射电源形态以及缺乏明亮X射线发射的现象。

Abstract: We present a multiwavelength analysis of the radio transient VT J1906+0849, discovered as a 70 mJy source in Epoch 1 of the Very Large Array Sky Survey (VLASS), 21 yr after an NRAO VLA Sky Survey (NVSS) non-detection. Radio observations reveal the source was first detected in 2005, peaking at $\gtrsim200$ mJy in 2014, then declining until a late 2025 rebrightening. The transient sits at a Galactic latitude of $\approx0.74^\circ$ and the properties of its optical-infrared counterpart support a Galactic origin. At $d\gtrsim15$ kpc, the extreme radio luminosity is likely powered by sustained accretion onto a compact object. However, a Swift-XRT non-detection shows it is X-ray faint relative to the Galactic X-ray binary population, and the radio emission is distinct from X-ray binaries in its temporal and spectral behavior. Broadband radio spectra suggest synchrotron self-absorption, but size constraints from equipartition and very long baseline interferometry show little to no expansion in the radio-emitting region over 5+ yr, despite significant spectral evolution. Near-infrared spectroscopy reveals a single broad emission line with a stable centroid but variable width and luminosity. We attribute this feature to blueshifted Br$γ$ tracing a persistent asymmetric $\approx2000$ km s$^{-1}$ outflow. These properties are unlike any previously identified Galactic radio source. One possible interpretation is that VT J1906+0849 is a young analog of the microquasar SS 433, with a dense disk wind confining a continuously powered synchrotron outflow. This jet-wind interaction explains the compact, slowly expanding radio source and may contribute to the absence of bright X-ray emission.

Walding,M. Ward,S. Westerdale,R. Wormington*

TL;DR: 本文报道了利用DEAP-3600实验在加拿大SNOLAB地下2公里处对宇宙线缪子通量的直接测量。通过符合分析（缪子符合探测器与液氩靶同时触发）以及独立使用缪子符合探测器数据的两种方法，测得缪子通量为(3.71 ± 0.25stat ± 0.09sys) × 10⁻¹⁰ μ/cm²/s，与SNO实验的先前结果及MUTE软件模拟一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测量地下缪子通量对于暗物质直接探测等稀有事件搜索实验的背景估算至关重要。SNOLAB作为深地实验设施，需要精确的缪子通量基准数据来支持未来稀有事件搜索实验的设计和运行。

Method: 利用DEAP-3600实验的缪子符合探测器（水罐）和液氩靶进行两类测量：(1) 符合分析——要求缪子同时穿过水罐和液氩靶，以最小化仪器本底和系统不确定性；(2) 独立分析——仅使用缪子符合探测器的cut-and-count分析。数据采集时间为2016年11月至2020年3月。

Result: 通过符合分析测得缪子通量为(3.71 ± 0.25stat ± 0.09sys) × 10⁻¹⁰ μ/cm²/s。仅使用缪子符合探测器的独立测量结果在不确定度范围内与符合分析一致。两项测量均与SNO实验的先前结果和MUTE软件模拟相符。

Conclusion: DEAP-3600对SNOLAB缪子通量的精确测量为未来稀有事件搜索实验提供了重要的基准参考，验证了该深度下缪子背景模型的可靠性，同时展示了不同测量方法之间以及实验结果与模拟之间的一致性。

Abstract: A direct measurement of the muon flux at SNOLAB is performed using the DEAP-3600 experiment, located 2 km underground at SNOLAB near Sudbury, Canada. Primarily designed for the direct detection of weakly interacting massive particles (WIMPs), a dark matter candidate, DEAP-3600 consists of an inner spherical acrylic vessel containing a liquid argon target; this vessel is enclosed within a steel shell which is submerged in an instrumented water tank, serving as a muon veto for the dark matter search. The muon flux measurement is performed using a cut-and-count analysis of events observed in the muon veto detector and of events which are coincident between the muon veto and the liquid argon target. The requirement that muons traverse both the water and liquid argon minimizes instrumental backgrounds and systematic uncertainties. Using data collected from November 2016 to March 2020, the muon flux is measured by this coincidence analysis to be $(3.71 \pm 0.25_{\textrm{stat}} \pm 0.09_{\textrm{sys}}) \times 10^{-10}\, μ/$cm$^2$/s. The standalone measurement using muon veto data only is compatible within uncertainties. Both measurements agree with the previous result by the SNO experiment and with simulations carried out using the MUTE software. These results provide an important benchmark for future rare-event searches at the SNOLAB facility.

</details>

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于BESIII探测器在BEPCII对撞机上采集的2.712×10^9个ψ(3686)事例，研究了ψ(3686)→γp p̄π+π-π0过程，发现ηc(2S)→p p̄π+π-π0衰变的证据（显著性3.3σ），首次测定了相关分支比乘积，并观测到χcJ(J=0,1,2)的强子衰变，测定了其分支比，同时以显著提高的精度更新了χcJ→p p̄ω的中间过程分支比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究粲偶素激发态（ψ(3686)、ηc(2S)、χcJ）的多体强子衰变和辐射衰变，以更深入理解QCD在粲偶素能区的物理机制，并提供更精确的测量数据用于检验理论模型。

Method: 利用BESIII探测器在BEPCII对撞机上采集的大样本ψ(3686)数据（2.712×10^9个事例），通过ψ(3686)→γp p̄π+π-π0过程，分析ηc(2S)的辐射衰变产物的强子衰变，以及χcJ(J=0,1,2)的强子衰变通道，并对χcJ→p p̄ω中间过程进行分支比测量。

Result: 发现ηc(2S)→p p̄π+π-π0的衰变证据（3.3σ），测得B[ψ(3686)→γηc(2S)]×B[ηc(2S)→p p̄π+π-π0]=(3.4±0.5±0.8)×10^-6；观测到χcJ→p p̄π+π-π0三个衰变并测得分支比分别为4.79×10^-3、2.13×10^-3和3.72×10^-3；更新了χcJ→p p̄ω的分支比，精度显著提高。

Conclusion: 该工作首次给出了ηc(2S)→p p̄π+π-π0衰变的证据和分支比信息，同时提供了χcJ→p p̄π+π-π0及χcJ→p p̄ω的精确测量结果，为粲偶素强子衰变的研究提供了重要的实验参考。

Abstract: Using $(2.712\pm0.014)\times 10^9$ $ψ(3686)$ events collected by the BESIII detector at the BEPCII collider, the $ψ(3686) \to γp\bar{p}π^+π^-π^0$ process is investigated. Evidence for the decay of $η_{c}(2S)\to p\bar{p}π^{+}π^{-}π^{0}$ is found with a signal significance of 3.3$σ$. The product of branching fractions of $\mathcal{B}[ψ(3686)\to γη_{c}(2S)]\times\mathcal{B}[η_{c}(2S)\to p\bar{p}π^{+}π^{-}π^{0}]$ is determined to be $(3.4\pm0.5\pm0.8) \times 10^{-6}$, where the first uncertainty is statistical and the second systematic. The hadronic decays of $χ_{cJ} \to p\bar{p}π^+π^-π^0$$~(J=0,1,2)$ are observed, and their branching fractions are measured to be $\mathcal{B}(χ_{c0}\to p\bar{p}π^{+}π^{-}π^{0})=(4.79\pm 0.01\pm0.40) \times 10^{-3}$, $\mathcal{B}(χ_{c1}\to p\bar{p}π^{+}π^{-}π^{0})=(2.13\pm 0.01\pm0.17) \times 10^{-3}$, and $\mathcal{B}(χ_{c2}\to p\bar{p}π^{+}π^{-}π^{0})=(3.72\pm 0.01\pm0.29) \times 10^{-3}$, respectively. Furthermore, the branching fractions for the intermediate processes $χ_{cJ}\to p\bar{p}ω$ are updated with significantly improved precision: $\mathcal{B}(χ_{c0}\to p\bar{p}ω)=(5.76\pm0.01\pm0.42)\times10^{-4}$, $\mathcal{B}(χ_{c1}\to p\bar{p}ω)=(1.85\pm0.01\pm0.13)\times10^{-4}$, and $\mathcal{B}(χ_{c2}\to p\bar{p}ω)=(4.51\pm0.01\pm0.33)\times10^{-4}$, respectively.

</details>

Zhang,J. Zhao,M. Zhao,Y. Zhao,A. Zhelezov,S. Z. Zheng,X. Z. Zheng,Y. Zheng,T. Zhou,X. Zhou,V. Zhovkovska,L. Z. Zhu,X. Zhu,X. Zhu,Y. Zhu,V. Zhukov,J. Zhuo,T. Zies,D. Zuliani,X. Zuo*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文首次对Λ_b^0 → Λ(1520)μ+μ-衰变进行了角分析，利用LHCb探测器2011-2018年采集的质子-质子碰撞数据（积分亮度9 fb^-1），测得轻子前后向不对称性A_FB和CP平均角观测量S_1cc，结果与标准模型预测一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Λ_b^0 → Λ(1520)μ+μ-衰变是稀有b→sll味改变中性流过程，对超出标准模型的新物理敏感。之前未对此特定衰变模式进行过角分析，因此本研究填补了这一空白，检验标准模型预言并寻找新物理迹象。

Method: 使用LHCb探测器在2011-2018年间收集的质子-质子对撞数据（积分亮度9 fb^-1）。通过拟合四个双缪子不变质量平方区间（0.1-12.5 GeV^2/c^4）内的角分布投影，确定轻子前后向不对称性A_FB,3/2^ℓ和CP平均角观测量S_1cc。

Result: 所测得的A_FB,3/2^ℓ和S_1cc在四个质量区间内的结果均与基于粒子物理标准模型的预言在误差范围内良好一致，未发现与标准模型的显著偏离。

Conclusion: 该结果为Λ_b^0 → Λ(1520)μ+μ-衰变提供了首次角分析测量，其结果支持标准模型，同时为后续更高统计量下的精确检验和新物理搜索奠定了实验基础。

Abstract: The first angular analysis of ${\it Λ}_{\it b}^{0} \to {\it Λ}(1520){\it μ^{+}μ^{-}}$ decays is presented, using proton-proton collision data collected with the LHCb detector between 2011 and 2018, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 9 fb$^{-1}$. The leptonic forward-backward asymmetry, $A_\text{FB, 3/2}^\ell$, and the $CP$-averaged angular observable, $S_{1cc}$, are determined by fitting projections of the angular distributions in four intervals of the square of the dimuon invariant mass between 0.1 and 12.5 GeV$^2/c^4$. The results are in good agreement with predictions based on the Standard Model of particle physics.

</details>


### [102] [New Limits on $n \rightarrow n'$ Transformation from HFIR Cold Neutron Beam](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21120)
*James M. Rogers,Leah J. Broussard,Christopher B. Crawford,Lisa DeBeer-Schmitt,Matthew J. Frost,Francisco M. Gonzalez,Carolyn O. Haviland,Lawrence Heilbronn,Erik B. Iverson,Yuri Kamyshkov,Mubasshir Khan,Andrew Mullins,David Milstead,Linus B. Persson,Cary Rock,Valentina Santoro,Alexander Saunders,Shaun Vavra,Nathan D. Whittington*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文报告了一项新的中子到无菌中子（n→n'）转化实验搜寻，使用橡树岭国家实验室高通量同位素反应堆的强冷中子束、6.6 T超导磁体及薄的镉吸收片，未观察到高于背景的信号，将真空中的转化概率下限提高了约一个数量级（Δm在0.1–1000 neV范围）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 假设性的中子到无菌中子的转化会违反重子数B，并可能揭示暗物质的本质。这一过程对理解超越标准模型的物理具有重要意义，因此值得进行高灵敏度实验搜寻。

Method: 使用高通量同位素反应堆的强冷中子束，基于双参数理论模型（质量差Δm和混合真空角θ0），在超导6.6 T磁体中心放置薄的吸收性镉片以产生大梯度，引发非绝热n→n'转变；在下游20米处用³He中子探测器进行信号探测。

Result: 在探测器上未观察到底噪之上的信号；该结果将真空中n→n'转化概率2θ0²的下限在Δm介于0.1 neV与1000 neV范围内提高了约一个数量级。

Conclusion: 未发现中子到无菌中子转化的证据，但显著改进了对这类过程的实验约束，为暗物质本质和重子数破坏物理提供了更严格的限制。

Abstract: Hypothetical neutron $n$ to sterile neutron $n'$ transformations would violate baryon number $\mathcal{B}$ and point to the nature of Dark Matter. We performed a new search for $n \rightarrow n'$ using an intense cold neutron beam from the High Flux Isotope Reactor at Oak Ridge National Laboratory. We used a theoretical model that describes the transformation $n \rightarrow n'$ with two parameters: a small mass difference $Δ{m}$ between the interaction states $n$ and $n'$ and a mixing vacuum angle $θ_0$. A thin absorbing cadmium wafer was used in the center of the superconducting 6.6 T magnet which provided a large gradient for the non-adiabatic $n \rightarrow n'$ transition. No signal was observed above background in the $^{3}\text{He}$ neutron detector 20 meters downstream of the magnet. This result gives an order of magnitude improvement in the lower limit for the probability $2θ_0^2$ of the $n \rightarrow n'$ transformation in vacuum in the range of $Δ{m}$ between $0.1$ neV and $1000$ neV.

</details>


### [103] [Development of A Novel Compton Camera for MeV Gamma-Ray Measurement in Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21216)
*Pingwei Sun,Wenxiang Fang,Guorong He,Zhen Wu,Jinghe Yang,Jiancheng Zeng,Yiyu Pan,Jiacheng Ding,Enzhao Qi,Jiahao Su,Haoran Yang,Bowen Zhu,Mengjiao Xiao*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种基于新型闪烁体的三层康普顿相机，具有优于4%的能量分辨率和约2mm的位置分辨率，旨在填补MeV伽马射线"MeV间隙"的天文观测空白，并展示了其设计、标定和验证结果，证明其具备良好的MeV伽马射线源成像能力，可用于即将到来的在轨任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于过去几十年检测技术的局限性，MeV能量区的天体物理伽马射线尚未被充分探索，存在著名的伽马射线"MeV间隙"。打开MeV伽马射线窗口对伽马天文学及天体粒子物理前沿研究（如探测轻暗物质、探测原初黑洞、更好地理解核合成过程）至关重要。

Method: 采用新型闪烁体，研制了一种三层康普顿相机，该相机实现了优于4%的能量分辨率和约2mm的位置分辨率，并对其进行了详细的设计、标定和验证。

Result: 开发的康普顿相机在能量分辨率（优于4%）和位置分辨率（约2mm）方面均达到优良指标，验证结果表明其具备良好的MeV伽马射线源成像能力，可以为即将到来的在轨任务提供支持。

Conclusion: 该三层康普顿相机成功展示了其在MeV伽马射线源成像方面的能力，有望为填补天体物理中MeV伽马射线观测空白、促进暗物质探测和核合成研究等前沿科学做出贡献。

Abstract: The astrophysical gamma rays in the MeV energy region have not yet been well-explored due to the limitation of detection technology in the past decades, and the famous gamma-ray "MeV gap" exists. Opening the window of MeV gamma-ray is not only critical for the gamma astronomy but also essential for rich frontier researches in astro-particle physics, such as detecting light dark matter, probing the primordial black hole and better understanding of nucleosynthesis. Using the novel scintillators, a three-layer Compton camera with the energy resolution better than 4% and position resolution of ~2 mm is developed. Here we show the design, detailed calibration and validation results of the novel Compton camera, and demonstrate its good ability of MeV gamma-ray source imaging for the upcoming in-orbit mission.

</details>


### [104] [On-Detector Machine Learning for Beam-Induced Background Rejection at a 10 TeV Muon Collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21327)
*Daniel Abadjiev,Eliza Howard,Tsz Ngong You,Ryan Michaud,Benjamin Ryan Roberts,Benjamin Rosser,Karri Folan Di Petrillo,Anthony Badea,Doug Berry,Arghya Ranjan Das,Jennet Dickinson,Giuseppe Di Guglielmo,Harshul Gupta,Farah Fahim,Abhijith Gandrakota,Lindsey Gray,James Hirschauer,David Jiang,Shiqi Kuang,Ron Lipton,Mira Littmann,Miaoyuan Liu,Nicholas Manganelli,Petar Maksimovic,Corrinne Mills,Mark S. Neubauer,Aidan Nicholas,Benjamin Parpillon,Jannicke Pearkes,Adam Quinn,Danush Shekar,Ricardo Silvestre,Chinar Syal,Morris Swartz,Nhan Tran,Amit Trivedi,Keith Ulmer,Mohammad Abrar Wadud,Benjamin Weiss*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文研究了在10 TeV缪子对撞机顶点探测器中利用片上机器学习（高能级综合实现轻量级神经网络）进行束流本底（BIB）抑制，所选架构在99%信号效率下实现88-90%数据约简，硬件资源满足ASIC实现可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 10 TeV缪子对撞机中，缪子衰变产生强烈的束流本底（BIB），可能使探测器占用率过载并超过读出带宽限制。因此亟需在数据读出层面实现高效的BIB抑制策略，以满足未来缪子对撞机轨迹探测器读出需求。

Method: 利用像素簇形状特征区分背景与对撞产物，研究三类轻量级神经网络架构用于片上BIB拒绝，并通过高能级综合（HLS）评估其硬件实现可行性。

Result: 所选架构在99%信号效率下实现88%至90%的数据约简，所需硬件资源与潜在ASIC实现兼容。

Conclusion: 研究证明了直接在像素读出中执行实质性BIB抑制的可行性，为未来缪子对撞机轨迹探测器满足读出需求提供了策略。

Abstract: A 10 TeV Muon Collider is a compelling candidate for a future energy-frontier facility, offering unprecedented opportunities to explore the fundamental laws of particle physics. Muon decays in the collider ring produce intense beam-induced background (BIB) that can overwhelm detector occupancy and exceed readout bandwidth constraints. We investigate the potential of on-detector Machine Learning for BIB rejection in the vertex detector, exploiting pixel cluster shapes to distinguish background from collision products. We study three classes of lightweight neural-network architectures, and evaluate their implementation feasibility using high-level synthesis. Selected architectures achieve 88 to 90% data reduction at 99% signal efficiency, while requiring hardware resources compatible with potential ASIC implementation. These results demonstrate the potential of performing substantial BIB rejection directly in the pixel readout, providing a strategy for meeting the tracker readout requirements at a future Muon Collider.

</details>
